# pg-cdc Milestone 3 (Park) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Park every event the sink cannot take within `delivery.park_after`, every event it rejects for good and every oversize event without a payload store in a durable StateStore (filesystem or S3), so the slot keeps advancing during a sink outage; redeliver parked queues in id order once the sink recovers; load parked queue heads by partition at startup before delivery for a key begins; and send oversize events as pointers to a PayloadStore.

**Architecture:** A `StateStore` trait (spec §11.3) with filesystem, S3 and in-memory implementations shares one conformance suite; a `PayloadStore` trait has an S3 implementation. The session hands both to the lanes. Each lane stays the single owner of its order keys (§8): besides milestone 2's live queues it now tracks, per key, whether the store holds a queue for it. A key that has been failing for `park_after`, or whose head the sink rejects for good, appends its in-memory events to the store (the head as `dead` when rejected) and settles them from the store's confirmation; while a key has a stored queue, new events are appended behind it, and the lane drains the queue head by head, removing each delivered head before reading the next and returning the key to the live path once the store reports the queue empty. A loader task lists the 256 partitions with bounded concurrency and hands each lane the heads of its keys before it releases the partition, so no live event overtakes a parked one. Oversize heads are written to the PayloadStore and replaced by a pointer event. On shutdown the session drains for 5 s, then asks every lane to park what remains.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 1.99.0 (edition 2024), tokio 1.53 (`JoinSet`, `Semaphore`, `mpsc`), async-trait 0.1.92, serde_json 1.0.154 (`raw_value` feature added), aws-sdk-s3 1.152 over rustls (ring), `std::fs::File::lock` for the filesystem leader lock, testcontainers 0.28 with moto 5.2.4 (R3), proptest 1.12, cargo-nextest, mise.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md` (binding). This plan implements spec §19 item 3 (the StateStore trait and its conformance suite, the filesystem and S3 stores; parking, dead events, draining and partitioned head loading; the PayloadStore and oversize pointers) using §1, §2.1, §4 (criteria 1, 3 and 6), §5, §7.1 (`payload_ref`), §7.5, §8, §9.1, §9.4, §10.2–§10.6, §11.3–§11.6, §12.5 step 2, §14 (O2, O3, A1, A2), §14.6, §15, §16.2 (the store check gating health, in its milestone 3 form), §18.2 and §18.4. It also folds in three milestone 2 follow-ups (Tasks 6 and 7).

## Global Constraints

Every task's requirements implicitly include this section.

- The spec is the binding authority. Where this plan and the spec disagree, the rulings below record why. Rulings R1–R50 of milestones 1 and 2 (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-m1-stream.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-m2-deliver.md`) stay in force except where a ruling below supersedes one by number; R33 is superseded by this milestone. Where the merged code and a plan's text differ, the code wins.
- The repository is public and open source, licensed `MIT OR Apache-2.0`. CI runs on pull requests from forks without any secret, and no AWS account or secret exists locally or in CI: every S3, SNS and SQS test runs against moto from `pg-cdc-testkit` (R3).
- Toolchain: Rust `1.99.0` pinned in `rust-toolchain.toml`; edition `2024`; workspace `rust-version = "1.99"`. In any worktree, run `mise trust && mise install` once before using mise. Rust 1.99 deprecates `AtomicU32::fetch_update`; use `try_update`.
- Run `mise run fmt` before `mise run check`. Code in this plan is rustfmt-formatted; diffs are unified diffs against the file as the previous task leaves it.
- `mise run check` is the single gate (lint-code, then test, then lint-deps): `cargo fmt --all --check`; `taplo fmt --check`; `typos`; `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`; `RUSTDOCFLAGS="-D warnings" cargo doc --workspace --no-deps --all-features`; `cargo nextest run --workspace --all-features`; `cargo deny check`; `cargo machete`. The last step of every task runs it and it must pass. If it outlives the 10-minute Bash timeout, run it in the background with output in `/Users/rajpatel/pg-cdc-work/integration/.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-m3-park/<task>-check.log` and poll the log.
- Lints live in `[workspace.lints]`; never weaken one. An exception is `#[expect(<lint>, reason = "...")]` at the narrowest scope; this plan needs none.
- Clippy's test exemptions apply inside `#[test]` functions and `#[cfg(test)]` items only. Helpers in `crates/*/tests/*.rs` outside a `#[test]` function may `assert!` (as `sink_conformance.rs` does) but never `unwrap`, `expect`, `panic!`, or index or slice a `Vec` or slice; a constant, in-bounds index or range into a fixed-size array (the conformance suite's `sorted[1..]`), which clippy's `indexing_slicing` accepts and which cannot panic, is allowed; a helper that can fail returns `TestResult` (R48).
- Clippy pedantic is on: keep functions under 100 lines, convert numbers with `try_from` or `u64::from`, never `as`, and never index or slice outside tests.
- Zero code comments, doc comments included. The only allowed comment states a genuine exception or anomaly the code cannot express; this plan needs none.
- Errors: `thiserror` in libraries, `anyhow` only in `crates/pg-cdc/src/main.rs`. No panics outside tests.
- Async: tokio only; no blocking calls in async code (filesystem store IO runs in `spawn_blocking`); never hold a lock across `.await` (the in-memory store and test doubles lock only inside synchronous sections; semaphore permits are not locks). Logs go through `tracing`.
- Bound every store fan-out: each S3 request takes a permit from the store's semaphore of `state_store.max_concurrency` (default 32), list pages included; head loading runs at most that many partitions at once; a lane holds at most one sink batch of parked heads in memory (R54, R60).
- Secrets: never commit one; never log a password, token or AWS credential.
- TLS is rustls with the ring provider only; every AWS client is built with `aws_smithy_http_client::Builder::new().tls_provider(Provider::Rustls(CryptoMode::Ring))`.
- Dependencies: no new third-party crate. `aws-sdk-s3` (already in `[workspace.dependencies]` for the testkit) becomes a `crates/pg-cdc` dependency, and the workspace `serde_json` gains the `raw_value` feature.
- Fixed values: park and drain store backoff 100 ms to 30 s with full jitter (`lane::RETRY_BASE`, `lane::RETRY_CAP`); 256 partitions (`state::PARTITIONS`); S3 client standard retries with 3 attempts, 10 s per attempt, 30 s per operation; drain 5 s, then park for at most another 5 s (`session::DRAIN_TIMEOUT`). Defaults: `delivery.park_after: 30s`, `delivery.max_parked_events: 10000000` (`0` unlimited), `state_store.max_concurrency: 32`.
- Object layout (spec §11.4–§11.6): `<prefix>/<pipeline>/parked/<order key id>/<event id>.json`, `<prefix>/<pipeline>/leader.json`, `<prefix>/<pipeline>/payloads/<event id>.json`; the filesystem store keeps the same tree under `state_store.path`.
- pg-cdc never creates, drops or advances a slot or publication except through standby status updates (S1). Only tests create them, through `pg-cdc-testkit`.
- Tests run with cargo-nextest. Unit tests sit beside the code; integration tests live in `crates/*/tests` and use `pg-cdc-testkit` (Docker required). `PGCDC_TEST_PG_VERSION` selects Postgres 14 to 18 and defaults to 17. Tests are deterministic and print nothing on success; time-based unit tests use `#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]`. A red run of a property test may leave files under `crates/pg-cdc/proptest-regressions/`; delete new ones before committing.
- Coverage floors, enforced by `mise run cov`: `pg-cdc-decoder` at least 90% of lines; the workspace at least 80% of lines excluding `crates/testkit` and `crates/loadgen`.
- Shared files: tasks in parallel lanes edit the same manifests (the root `Cargo.toml`, `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml`, `Cargo.lock`). Keep every dependency table in alphabetical order, add only the lines the task needs, and when a merge conflicts in a manifest, keep the union of both sides and regenerate `Cargo.lock` with `cargo metadata --format-version 1 > /dev/null` rather than hand-merging it. Every source file is owned by one task at a time; the task graph orders the tasks that share one.
- Commits: conventional commits, small and focused, on the task's own branch `task/m3-t<N>`. Every commit message ends with exactly:

  ```
  Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
  Claude-Session: https://claude.ai/code/session_0111KrCFecpy7EHrGXLaNCHr
  ```

  Never push, never force-push, never touch `/Users/rajpatel/pg-cdc` or `main`.

## Rulings

Each ruling is "decision — why — cost if wrong". Numbering continues from milestone 2.

- **R51. The stores reach the session before the lanes use them.** Task 7 threads `Stores` and `LaneSettings` through `run_session`, the supervisor and the binary while the lanes keep milestone 2 behavior (`Lanes::spawn` takes the stores as `_stores`); Tasks 8–10 then change only the lane module. This keeps the session, supervisor and binary out of the three lane tasks and lets Task 7 run as soon as the stores and config exist. Cost if wrong: one parameter unused for one task.
- **R52. A parked event stores the encoded message, not only the envelope.** §11.3 lists "the encoded envelope"; §7.5 and §11.1 define the encoded message as body plus attributes, and redelivery needs both. `ParkedEvent` therefore carries `attributes` and `body`; the stored JSON object embeds the envelope verbatim through `serde_json::value::RawValue` (so `serde_json` gains `raw_value`), keeps attributes as `{name, type, value}` objects, and records `first_failure_ms` as Unix milliseconds. A body that is not JSON cannot be stored (`StoreError::Malformed`); every body pg-cdc builds is JSON. Cost if wrong: a format change before any production data exists.
- **R53. A settled event has three proofs.** `Ticket::settle` takes a sink `Outcome` and settles only on `Delivered` (milestone 2); `Ticket::park` takes the store's append result and settles only on `Ok`; `Ticket::skip_replayed(id, floor)` settles only when `id < floor`, the §10.6 case of a replayed event below its queue's head that "settled before the restart". Nothing else builds a `Settled` (A1, §14.6). Cost if wrong: none.
- **R54. "Heads load one partition at a time" means the partition is the unit of loading and enabling.** Up to `state_store.max_concurrency` partitions load at once; each S3 request, list pages included, takes a permit from the store's semaphore of the same size; a partition's heads reach their lanes before that partition's `Loaded` signal, and no key of a partition is sent, drained or parked before it. Loading retries each partition with the store backoff until it succeeds. Cost if wrong: strictly sequential loading would slow startup by up to 256 store round trips.
- **R55. `state_store` is required; `payload_store` is optional.** The store is a runtime dependency (§2.1 goal 3) and milestone 4's leader record needs it (§12.4). `state_store: { type: filesystem, path }` serves development. `MemoryStateStore` exists for tests, passes the conformance suite, and is not configurable because it is not durable across processes. Cost if wrong: development configs need two more lines.
- **R56. A state store check gates the session like sink preflight (R40).** Before opening the replication connection, `pg-cdc run` retries `read_leader` (an absent record is fine) with the reconnect backoff until it succeeds or a signal arrives, so a task with a misconfigured bucket never takes the slot (§13.2, §16.2). The PayloadStore has only `put` (§11.6), so it has no check; a misconfigured payload bucket shows as retries and then parked events. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R57. Store failures back off the lane's store work; S3 clients retry 5xx themselves.** A failed append, read, remove or status change sets the lane's store backoff (100 ms to 30 s, full jitter, the §10.1 values) and is retried; sink retries are unaffected. The S3 client uses the SDK's standard retry mode with 3 attempts, which handles `503 SlowDown` and other 5xx with backoff (§11.4), unlike the sink, whose SDK retries stay disabled (§11.1). Each S3 attempt has a 10 s timeout and each operation 30 s. Cost if wrong: retry timing only.
- **R58. Parking is one-way until the store answers, and the parked limit defers only appends.** Once a key starts parking, its events are no longer sent live; a failed append is retried. At `delivery.max_parked_events` appends are deferred on their own timer while removals, status changes and head reads continue, so draining queues can free room; when the store then reports that a parking key has no queue, the key returns to live retries. Undeliverable events therefore "stay in flight" (§10.5), and the reader stops at the byte bound. Cost if wrong: none; the alternative deadlocks the limit (found while assembling this plan).
- **R59. `park_after` is a deadline, not a retry count.** A live key's retry timer is capped at first failure + `park_after`, so an event parks within one timer tick of the deadline rather than up to 30 s later. Redelivery failures of a parked head back off (§10.3) and never re-park. `attempts` and `last_error` are recorded when the event parks and are not rewritten on every drain failure, so the store pays one write per status change (§11.4). Cost if wrong: stored attempt counts undercount drain retries.
- **R60. A lane holds at most one sink batch of parked heads.** It reads up to `max_batch_messages` heads concurrently (one `read_parked(key, None, 1)` per key), sends them in batches like live heads, removes the delivered heads in one `remove_parked` call, and only then reads each key's next head. A head that fails is dropped from memory and re-read after its backoff. Memory for draining is bounded by `delivery.lanes` x `max_batch_messages` x `max_message_bytes`, and an event is never resent because a removal failed (the key waits for the removal). Cost if wrong: drain throughput per lane is one batch per store round trip.
- **R61. A stored key's floor is its last known head.** It comes from head loading, a head read, a fresh park, or the last removed id; replayed events below it settle through `skip_replayed` without being sent (§10.6), and any other event for the key is appended (create-only). A replay that arrives after its key's queue has fully drained is sent again, in order: a duplicate, never a loss or reordering. Cost if wrong: duplicates under at-least-once delivery.
- **R62. Oversize pointers.** The threshold is the sink's `max_message_bytes` against `Outbound::encoded_len` (body plus attributes, §7.5). With a PayloadStore, the head's body is `put` and replaced by a pointer that keeps the attributes and omits `before`, `after` and `unchanged_toast` entirely (absent, not `null`), with `payload_ref` placed before `source`; it is built by re-serializing the encoded body, whose `preserve_order` and `arbitrary_precision` features keep field order and key values exact. A `put` failure is a retryable failure of the key (so after `park_after` the full envelope parks); a pointer still above the limit, or a body that is not a JSON object, parks dead. A drained oversize head is offloaded the same way. Payloads are written with a plain PUT, so a replay overwrites identical bytes. Without a PayloadStore the event parks dead (§7.5). Cost if wrong: none.
- **R63. Parking the remainder at shutdown (§12.5 step 2) lands in this milestone, because it is parking.** After `DRAIN_TIMEOUT` (5 s) the session asks every lane to park what remains (`Lanes::park_all`) and waits at most another `DRAIN_TIMEOUT`; lanes stop sending and loading once asked. What neither delivered nor parked replays, as in milestone 2. Milestone 4 adds `leadership.drain_timeout` and the leader record; 10 s fits ECS `stopTimeout` of at least 15 s (§12.7). Cost if wrong: a shutdown with a failing sink takes up to 10 s.
- **R64. The parked count is per session and approximate.** It starts from the loaded heads' queue lengths, grows by each appended batch (create-only no-ops included) and shrinks by each removal, so replays can over-count until the next restart; the limit therefore errs towards stalling early. `0` means unlimited. Cost if wrong: the limit trips slightly early after a restart with replays.
- **R65. S3 details.** `If-None-Match: *` returning 412 means the object exists and counts as success; a `NoSuchKey` code means a missing object, while a 404 `NoSuchBucket` is an error, so a missing bucket never looks like an empty store; a leader swap answered with 412, or with `NoSuchKey` under `If-Match`, is a conflict unless the stored record already equals the one being written (a retried write that landed); a 409 `ConditionalRequestConflict` is a retryable error. The SDK falls back to path-style addressing for IP endpoints, so the binary works against moto with `AWS_ENDPOINT_URL`. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R66. Filesystem details.** Append writes a temp file `.tmp-<name>-<random>`, fsyncs it, `hard_link`s it to the final name (an existing name is success), and fsyncs the directory and every newly created ancestor; temp files and other names are ignored when listing; a status change replaces the file through `rename`; an emptied key directory is removed (an append that races the removal retries); the leader record is `{"version": n, "record": {...}}` replaced under `std::fs::File::lock` on `leader.lock`. Blocking IO runs in `spawn_blocking` behind a semaphore of `max_concurrency`. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R67. Milestone 2 follow-up: `limit_from_topic` reports whether the topic's size was used.** `Topic` records `declared_size` (a parsed, positive `MaximumMessageSize`), and preflight logs it; the field is not named `limit_from_topic` because clippy's `struct_field_names` rejects a field ending in its struct's name. The parked real-AWS check of the `PublishBatch` aggregate limit above 256 KiB relies on this signal. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R68. Milestone 2 follow-up: String attribute values with characters SNS and SQS reject are omitted.** Any String attribute (core or `attribute_columns`) containing a character outside #x9, #xA, #xD, #x20–#xD7FF, #xE000–#xFFFD and #x10000–#x10FFFF is omitted with a warning naming the event and attribute, like a null (§11.2, amended by one sentence in Task 6); otherwise such a row would become a dead event once parking exists. Message bodies are unaffected: serde_json escapes control characters, and a body the sink still rejects parks dead. Cost if wrong: a consumer filtering on that attribute misses such rows.
- **R69. Milestone 2 follow-up: every ordering and lane failure is pinned as fatal.** Task 7 pins `is_fatal` for `Lane(OutOfOrder)`, `LaneStopped` and `LanesStopped` (and keeps `Ack`), and pins that the supervisor stops at once on each, so the O2 fail-stop cannot quietly become a reconnect loop. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R70. Event ids and order key ids parse from their text forms.** Store keys must be read back, so `EventId` and `OrderKeyId` implement `FromStr` for exactly their own lowercase hex forms, and `OrderKeyId::partition` returns the first byte (the first two hex digits). Cost if wrong: none.
- **R71. The lane test harness lets startup loading finish.** `Harness::new` waits 1 ms of paused time after spawning, so an empty store's 256 partitions load before a test sends; the milestone 2 lane tests assert batch order across keys, and keys in different partitions are released in partition-load order. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R72. Milestone 2 tests that pinned R33 are rewritten, not deleted silently.** `a_terminal_outcome_blocks_its_key_and_never_settles` becomes `a_terminal_outcome_parks_the_event_dead_and_blocks_its_key`; `an_event_larger_than_the_sink_message_limit_is_never_sent` becomes `an_event_above_the_sink_limit_is_parked_dead_without_a_payload_store`; the terminal property test becomes the model test of Task 9; `retries_back_off_with_full_jitter_up_to_thirty_seconds` sets `park_after` to an hour; `shutdown_with_a_failing_sink_stops_cleanly_and_replays_the_undelivered_event` keeps its replay assertion with a store that refuses appends; the CLI test `sigterm_after_stdout_closes_exits_cleanly_and_confirms_only_what_was_delivered` becomes `sigterm_after_stdout_closes_parks_the_undelivered_event_and_exits_cleanly`. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R73. Deferred.** The real-AWS check of the SNS `PublishBatch` aggregate limit above 256 KiB and of whether `GetTopicAttributes` returns `MaximumMessageSize` stays a pre-production staging check, like the Aurora failover row of §21. Operator redrive and discard (§10.4, §16.2) are milestone 5; until then an operator removes a dead event by stopping pg-cdc and deleting its object, which the README documents. The model test covers random failures and restarts; operator actions join it with the admin API. Metrics and spans (§17) are milestone 5. Cost if wrong: none.
- **R74. New stores are one implementation.** A backend implements `StateStore` (or `PayloadStore`), adds a config variant and a match arm in `main.rs`, and adds a test file that includes the conformance suite with `#[path = "support/state_store.rs"] mod state_store;` and calls `state_store::conforms` with a function that opens a fresh handle onto the same backing data. The roadmap's Postgres store (§20) is held to the same suite. Cost if wrong: none.

## Review Focus

The five inputs or failure modes the spec implies but its listed tests do not exercise, most likely to bite first. Each is pinned by a test in the owning task.

1. **The sink and the state store are down at the same time, or the store returns while the sink is still down.** Expected: nothing settles and nothing is lost while both are down; events stay in flight until backpressure stops the reader; parking resumes as soon as the store answers, and a shutdown in that state exits 0 and replays. Tests: Task 9 `a_store_outage_keeps_parking_events_in_flight_until_it_ends`, Task 8 `a_store_outage_while_draining_resends_nothing_and_resumes`, Task 11 `shutdown_with_a_failing_sink_and_store_stops_cleanly_and_replays_the_undelivered_event`.
2. **A crash after events were parked but before their transaction was confirmed, followed by a replay of the same events.** Expected: replayed events below a queue's head settle without being sent, replayed events already in the queue are not duplicated, nothing of a key is sent before its partition's heads load, and blocked keys stay blocked. Tests: Task 8 `replayed_events_below_the_head_settle_without_being_sent` and `o3_a_partition_delivers_nothing_until_its_heads_load`, Task 9 `o2_o3_a1_lanes_park_and_drain_under_random_failures_and_restarts`, Task 11 `shutdown_parks_what_the_sink_did_not_take_and_the_next_session_drains_it`.
3. **A long outage leaves tens of thousands of parked keys, and pg-cdc restarts.** Expected: S3 requests stay at `state_store.max_concurrency` during head loading, listing and fan-out writes; listing pages through more than 1,000 objects; a lane holds at most one batch of parked heads in memory. Tests: Task 3 `s3_requests_never_exceed_max_concurrency` and `heads_load_across_listing_pages`, Task 8 `startup_loading_is_bounded_by_the_store_concurrency` and `parked_heads_in_memory_never_exceed_one_batch`.
4. **A wide row (a large `jsonb` or text column) above the topic's limit, with or without a working payload bucket.** Expected: with a payload store the sink gets a pointer whose `payload_ref` names a readable object; when the bucket is missing the event retries and then parks whole; a pointer still too large, or no payload store, parks the event dead and the slot moves on. Tests: Task 10 `an_oversize_event_is_sent_as_a_pointer_to_its_stored_payload`, `a_payload_store_outage_retries_and_then_parks_the_full_event` and `a_pointer_still_above_the_limit_is_parked_dead`, Task 10 session tests `an_oversize_row_reaches_the_sink_as_a_pointer_to_its_payload` and `without_a_payload_store_an_oversize_row_is_parked_dead`, Task 4 `a_missing_bucket_reports_the_payload_store_unavailable`.
5. **A deploy with a mistyped state bucket.** Expected: the new task logs the store check failure, never takes the slot, and exits 0 on SIGTERM, so a start-first deploy keeps the old task running. Tests: Task 7 `a_missing_state_bucket_keeps_the_store_check_retrying_without_taking_the_slot`, Task 3 `a_missing_bucket_reports_the_store_unavailable`.

| A1 only delivery or parking settles | `a1_a_park_settles_a_ticket_only_once_the_store_confirms_it` and `a1_a_replayed_event_settles_only_below_its_queues_floor` (8); `a_store_outage_keeps_parking_events_in_flight_until_it_ends` and the model test (9) |
| A2 confirmed LSN is safe | milestone 2's ack tests keep running; `parking_lets_the_slot_advance_while_the_sink_fails_and_drains_in_order` and `a_rejected_event_is_parked_dead_and_blocks_only_its_key` (9); `shutdown_parks_what_the_sink_did_not_take_and_the_next_session_drains_it` (11) |

A3, A4, D1–D4 and S1 are unchanged and their milestone 1 and 2 tests keep running. L1–L3 belong to milestone 4; Task 1 already pins the leader record's compare-and-swap (contract item 6) in the conformance suite.

## File Structure

```
Cargo.toml                                    serde_json + raw_value                                       (T1)
crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml  Cargo.lock          + aws-sdk-s3                                                 (T3; T4 if first)
crates/pg-cdc/src/lib.rs                      + pub mod payload, pub mod state                             (T1)
crates/pg-cdc/src/event/id.rs  event/mod.rs   EventId: FromStr, InvalidEventId, lower_hex                  (T1)
crates/pg-cdc/src/order.rs                    OrderKeyId: FromStr, partition, InvalidOrderKeyId            (T1)
crates/pg-cdc/src/state/mod.rs                StateStore, ParkedEvent, ParkedHead, LeaderRecord, codec     (T1)
crates/pg-cdc/src/state/memory.rs             MemoryStateStore                                             (T1)
crates/pg-cdc/src/state/filesystem.rs         FilesystemStateStore                                         (T1 empty; T2)
crates/pg-cdc/src/state/s3.rs                 S3StateStore, s3_client                                      (T1 empty; T3)
crates/pg-cdc/src/payload.rs                  PayloadStore, PayloadRef, S3PayloadStore, pointer_body       (T1 empty; T4)
```

## Task Graph

| Task | Title | Depends on | Complexity | Risk |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | State store contract and conformance suite | none | complex | high |
| 2 | Filesystem state store | 1 | complex | high |
| 3 | S3 state store | 1 | complex | high |
| 4 | Payload store and pointer events | 1 | standard | high |
| 5 | Parking and store configuration | none | standard | normal |
| 6 | SNS sink follow-ups | none | standard | normal |
| 7 | Stores in the session, supervisor and binary | 2, 3, 4, 5 | standard | high |
| 8 | Lanes drain parked queues | 1, 7 | complex | high |
| 9 | Lanes park failing and rejected events | 8 | complex | high |
| 10 | Oversize events as pointers | 4, 9 | standard | high |
| 11 | Park the remainder at shutdown, end to end | 7, 10 | standard | high |

Parallel lanes: Tasks 1, 5 and 6 first; after Task 1, Tasks 2, 3 and 4 together; Task 7 once 2, 3, 4 and 5 are in; then Tasks 8, 9, 10 and 11 in order (they share the lane module and the stream test files).

Verification of this plan: the code of every task was assembled, in task order, on an export of the integration branch at `0eeb6ad` with Rust 1.99.0. At every task boundary `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings` and the whole workspace's nextest suite passed (268 tests after Task 1, 319 after Task 11, Postgres 17, moto 5.2.4), and the final tree passed `mise run check` and `mise run cov` (workspace 96.0% of lines, decoder 98.1%). Mutation checks: removing the S3 request permit fails `s3_requests_never_exceed_max_concurrency`; an unbounded head capacity fails `parked_heads_in_memory_never_exceed_one_batch`; removing partition gating fails `o3_a_partition_delivers_nothing_until_its_heads_load` and `replayed_events_below_the_head_settle_without_being_sent`. The model test passed 3,000 cases; the lane, ack and supervisor unit tests ran 60 times and the S3, filesystem, payload, stream and CLI suites three times without a failure. Assembling the plan found and fixed three defects that its tests now pin: a missing bucket read as an empty store (R65), the parked limit deadlocking draining (R58), and a jitter-sensitive store-outage test.

---

### Task 1: State store contract and conformance suite

**Depends on:** none
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Implements `pg_cdc::state` (spec §11.3): the object-safe `StateStore` trait with the exact §11.3 signatures (plus `Debug` as a supertrait, like `Sink`, R36), the parked-event, head and leader-record types, the JSON codec the filesystem and S3 stores share (R52), object names for the §11.4 layout, and `MemoryStateStore`, an in-process store for tests (R55). The conformance suite checks the seven contract items against any store through a function that opens fresh handles onto the same data; the memory store passes it here, and Tasks 2 and 3 run it against the filesystem and S3 stores (R74). `EventId` and `OrderKeyId` learn to parse their text forms, and `OrderKeyId::partition` returns the first byte (R70). The empty `state/filesystem.rs`, `state/s3.rs` and `payload.rs` files are created now so that Tasks 2, 3 and 4 never edit `lib.rs` or `state/mod.rs` and can run in parallel lanes.

**Files:**
- Modify: `Cargo.toml` (workspace `serde_json` gains `raw_value`)
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lib.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/id.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/event/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/order.rs`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/state/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/state/memory.rs`
- Create (empty): `crates/pg-cdc/src/state/filesystem.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/state/s3.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/payload.rs`
- Test: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/support/state_store.rs` (the suite), `crates/pg-cdc/tests/state_memory.rs`; unit tests in `event/id.rs`, `order.rs` and `state/mod.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc::event::EventId::{new, commit_lsn, ordinal}`, `pg_cdc::order::OrderKeyId::{of, lane}`, `pg_cdc::sink::{Outbound, Attributes, AttributeValue::{data_type, as_str}}`, `pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn` (`Display` as `X/X`, `FromStr`) and `pg_cdc_decoder::lsn::ParseLsnError`.
- Produces:
  - `impl FromStr for EventId` (`Err = InvalidEventId`, accepting exactly 16 lowercase hex digits, `-`, 8 lowercase hex digits), `pub struct InvalidEventId(pub String)`, re-exported from `pg_cdc::event`; `pub(crate) fn lower_hex(text: &str, width: usize) -> bool` re-exported crate-wide from `event`.
  - `impl FromStr for OrderKeyId` (`Err = InvalidOrderKeyId`, exactly 32 lowercase hex digits), `pub struct InvalidOrderKeyId(pub String)`, `OrderKeyId::partition(self) -> u8`.
  - `pg_cdc::state`: `PARTITIONS: usize = 256`, `LEADER_OBJECT = "leader.json"`, `PARKED_DIR = "parked"`; `enum ParkStatus { Retrying, Dead }`; `struct ParkedEvent { order_key: OrderKeyId, id: EventId, status: ParkStatus, attempts: u32, first_failure_ms: u64, last_error: String, attributes: Attributes, body: Bytes }` with `fn outbound(&self) -> Outbound`; `struct ParkedHead { order_key: OrderKeyId, head: EventId, length: u64 }`; `enum LeaderState { Active, Released }`; `struct LeaderRecord { epoch: u64, holder: String, state: LeaderState, confirmed_lsn: Lsn, updated_ms: u64 }` (serde, LSN as text); `struct Version(pub String)`; `struct Versioned<T> { value: T, version: Version }`; `enum SwapOutcome { Swapped(Version), Conflict }`; `enum StoreError { Unavailable(String), NotFound(String), Malformed { key: String, reason: String } }` (`Clone + PartialEq`); the `StateStore` trait; `store_root(prefix: &str, pipeline: &str) -> String`, `parked_name(&OrderKeyId, &EventId) -> String` (`<key>/<id>.json`), `parse_parked_name(&str) -> Option<(OrderKeyId, EventId)>`, `now_ms() -> u64`, `encode_parked(&ParkedEvent) -> Result<Vec<u8>, StoreError>`, `decode_parked(name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<ParkedEvent, StoreError>`, `decode_leader(name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<LeaderRecord, StoreError>`.
  - `pg_cdc::state::memory::MemoryStateStore` (`Clone` shares the same data, `new()`, `parked_events() -> usize`).
  - The suite in `crates/pg-cdc/tests/support/state_store.rs`: `pub(crate) type TestResult<T = ()>`, `pub(crate) async fn conforms<F, Fut>(open: F) -> TestResult` where `F: Fn() -> Fut`, `Fut: Future<Output = TestResult<Arc<dyn StateStore>>>`, and helpers `key(partition: u8, n: u8) -> TestResult<OrderKeyId>`, `id(lsn: u64, ordinal: u32) -> EventId`, `event(OrderKeyId, EventId, ParkStatus) -> ParkedEvent`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The suite checks, in order: empty calls succeed; reads return ascending ids and honor `after` and `limit` (contract 4); appending an existing event changes nothing, not even its status (contract 3); heads are each queue's lowest id with its length, within the partition only (contract 4); writes made through one handle are visible through a freshly opened one (contracts 1 and 2); a status change rewrites one event and a missing event is `NotFound`; removal of a missing event succeeds and reads reflect removals (contract 5); the leader record is a compare-and-swap where `None` means create-if-absent (contract 6); and 16 concurrent overlapping appends and 8 concurrent swaps from the same version are safe, with exactly one swap winning (contract 7). Event bodies carry escapes, multi-byte characters and an arbitrary-precision number so codecs must round-trip them byte for byte.

`crates/pg-cdc/tests/support/state_store.rs` (new file):

```rust
use std::future::Future;
use std::sync::Arc;

use bytes::Bytes;
use pg_cdc::event::EventId;
use pg_cdc::order::OrderKeyId;
use pg_cdc::sink::AttributeValue;
use pg_cdc::state::{
    LeaderRecord, LeaderState, ParkStatus, ParkedEvent, ParkedHead, StateStore, StoreError,
    SwapOutcome, Version, Versioned,
};
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;

pub(crate) type TestResult<T = ()> = Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

pub(crate) async fn conforms<F, Fut>(open: F) -> TestResult
where
    F: Fn() -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = TestResult<Arc<dyn StateStore>>>,
{
    let store = open().await?;
    empty_calls_succeed(store.as_ref()).await?;
    reads_return_ascending_ids_and_respect_after_and_limit(store.as_ref()).await?;
    appends_are_create_only(store.as_ref()).await?;
    heads_are_the_lowest_id_per_queue_in_their_partition(store.as_ref()).await?;
    writes_are_durable_across_instances(&open).await?;
    status_changes_rewrite_one_event(store.as_ref(), &open).await?;
    removal_is_idempotent_and_reads_reflect_it(store.as_ref()).await?;
    the_leader_record_is_a_compare_and_swap(store.as_ref(), &open).await?;
    concurrent_calls_are_safe(&store).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(crate) fn key(partition: u8, n: u8) -> TestResult<OrderKeyId> {
    Ok(format!("{partition:02x}{}{n:02x}", "0".repeat(28)).parse()?)
}

pub(crate) fn id(lsn: u64, ordinal: u32) -> EventId {
    EventId::new(Lsn(lsn), ordinal)
}

pub(crate) fn event(key: OrderKeyId, id: EventId, status: ParkStatus) -> ParkedEvent {
    ParkedEvent {
        order_key: key,
        id,
        status,
        attempts: 2,
        first_failure_ms: 1_760_443_200_123,
        last_error: "Throttling: rate exceeded \u{2014} \"slow down\"".to_owned(),
        attributes: vec![
            (
                "schema".to_owned(),
                AttributeValue::String("public".to_owned()),
            ),
            (
                "table".to_owned(),
                AttributeValue::String("orders".to_owned()),
            ),
            ("op".to_owned(), AttributeValue::String("insert".to_owned())),
            (
                "amount".to_owned(),
                AttributeValue::Number("42.50".to_owned()),
            ),
        ],
        body: Bytes::from(format!(
            r#"{{"id":"{id}","after":{{"note":"café 😀 \"x\"","n":12345678901234567890.10}}}}"#
        )),
    }
}

fn leader(epoch: u64, holder: &str, state: LeaderState) -> LeaderRecord {
    LeaderRecord {
        epoch,
        holder: holder.to_owned(),
        state,
        confirmed_lsn: Lsn(0x1_0000_0000 + epoch),
        updated_ms: 1_760_443_200_000 + epoch,
    }
}

async fn empty_calls_succeed(store: &dyn StateStore) -> TestResult {
    store.append_parked(&[]).await?;
    store.remove_parked(&[]).await?;
    assert_eq!(store.read_parked(&key(0x10, 1)?, None, 10).await?, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(store.load_parked_heads(0x10).await?, vec![]);
    Ok(())
}

async fn reads_return_ascending_ids_and_respect_after_and_limit(
    store: &dyn StateStore,
) -> TestResult {
    let queue = key(0x20, 1)?;
    let events = [
        event(queue, id(5, 1), ParkStatus::Retrying),
        event(queue, id(3, 0), ParkStatus::Dead),
        event(queue, id(5, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying),
    ];
    store.append_parked(&events).await?;
    let sorted = [events[1].clone(), events[2].clone(), events[0].clone()];
    assert_eq!(store.read_parked(&queue, None, 10).await?, sorted);
    assert_eq!(store.read_parked(&queue, None, 2).await?, sorted[..2]);
    assert_eq!(
        store.read_parked(&queue, Some(&id(3, 0)), 10).await?,
        sorted[1..]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        store.read_parked(&queue, Some(&id(4, 0)), 1).await?,
        sorted[1..2]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        store.read_parked(&queue, Some(&id(5, 1)), 10).await?,
        vec![]
    );
    assert_eq!(store.read_parked(&key(0x20, 2)?, None, 10).await?, vec![]);
    Ok(())
}

async fn appends_are_create_only(store: &dyn StateStore) -> TestResult {
    let queue = key(0x30, 1)?;
    let original = event(queue, id(7, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying);
    store.append_parked(std::slice::from_ref(&original)).await?;
    let mut changed = event(queue, id(7, 0), ParkStatus::Dead);
    changed.attempts = 99;
    "changed".clone_into(&mut changed.last_error);
    let added = event(queue, id(8, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying);
    store.append_parked(&[changed, added.clone()]).await?;
    assert_eq!(
        store.read_parked(&queue, None, 10).await?,
        vec![original, added]
    );
    Ok(())
}

async fn heads_are_the_lowest_id_per_queue_in_their_partition(
    store: &dyn StateStore,
) -> TestResult {
    let first = key(0x40, 1)?;
    let second = key(0x40, 2)?;
    let elsewhere = key(0x41, 1)?;
    store
        .append_parked(&[
            event(second, id(9, 2), ParkStatus::Retrying),
            event(first, id(6, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying),
            event(first, id(2, 5), ParkStatus::Dead),
            event(elsewhere, id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying),
            event(second, id(9, 1), ParkStatus::Retrying),
            event(first, id(4, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying),
        ])
        .await?;
    let mut heads = store.load_parked_heads(0x40).await?;
    heads.sort_by_key(|head| head.order_key);
    assert_eq!(
        heads,
        vec![
            ParkedHead {
                order_key: first,
                head: id(2, 5),
                length: 3
            },
            ParkedHead {
                order_key: second,
                head: id(9, 1),
                length: 2
            },
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        store.load_parked_heads(0x41).await?,
        vec![ParkedHead {
            order_key: elsewhere,
            head: id(1, 0),
            length: 1
        }]
    );
    assert_eq!(store.load_parked_heads(0x42).await?, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(store.load_parked_heads(0x04).await?, vec![]);
    Ok(())
}

async fn writes_are_durable_across_instances<F, Fut>(open: &F) -> TestResult
where
    F: Fn() -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = TestResult<Arc<dyn StateStore>>>,
{
    let reopened = open().await?;
    let queue = key(0x20, 1)?;
    let ids: Vec<EventId> = reopened
        .read_parked(&queue, None, 10)
        .await?
        .iter()
        .map(|event| event.id)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(ids, vec![id(3, 0), id(5, 0), id(5, 1)]);
    let written = event(key(0x50, 1)?, id(1, 1), ParkStatus::Retrying);
    reopened
        .append_parked(std::slice::from_ref(&written))
        .await?;
    let again = open().await?;
    assert_eq!(
        again.read_parked(&key(0x50, 1)?, None, 10).await?,
        vec![written]
    );
    Ok(())
}

async fn status_changes_rewrite_one_event<F, Fut>(store: &dyn StateStore, open: &F) -> TestResult
where
    F: Fn() -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = TestResult<Arc<dyn StateStore>>>,
{
    let queue = key(0x60, 1)?;
    let head = event(queue, id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying);
    let next = event(queue, id(2, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying);
    store.append_parked(&[head.clone(), next.clone()]).await?;
    store
        .set_parked_status(&queue, &id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Dead)
        .await?;
    let dead = ParkedEvent {
        status: ParkStatus::Dead,
        ..head.clone()
    };
    assert_eq!(
        open().await?.read_parked(&queue, None, 10).await?,
        vec![dead, next.clone()]
    );
    store
        .set_parked_status(&queue, &id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying)
        .await?;
    assert_eq!(store.read_parked(&queue, None, 10).await?, vec![head, next]);
    let missing = store
        .set_parked_status(&queue, &id(9, 9), ParkStatus::Dead)
        .await;
    assert!(
        matches!(missing, Err(StoreError::NotFound(_))),
        "{missing:?}"
    );
    let missing = store
        .set_parked_status(&key(0x60, 2)?, &id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Dead)
        .await;
    assert!(
        matches!(missing, Err(StoreError::NotFound(_))),
        "{missing:?}"
    );
    Ok(())
}

async fn removal_is_idempotent_and_reads_reflect_it(store: &dyn StateStore) -> TestResult {
    let queue = key(0x70, 1)?;
    let other = key(0x70, 2)?;
    store
        .append_parked(&[
            event(queue, id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Dead),
            event(queue, id(2, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying),
            event(queue, id(3, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying),
            event(other, id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying),
        ])
        .await?;
    store
        .remove_parked(&[
            (queue, id(1, 0)),
            (queue, id(9, 9)),
            (key(0x70, 3)?, id(1, 0)),
        ])
        .await?;
    store.remove_parked(&[(queue, id(1, 0))]).await?;
    let left: Vec<EventId> = store
        .read_parked(&queue, None, 10)
        .await?
        .iter()
        .map(|event| event.id)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(left, vec![id(2, 0), id(3, 0)]);
    let mut heads = store.load_parked_heads(0x70).await?;
    heads.sort_by_key(|head| head.order_key);
    assert_eq!(
        heads,
        vec![
            ParkedHead {
                order_key: queue,
                head: id(2, 0),
                length: 2
            },
            ParkedHead {
                order_key: other,
                head: id(1, 0),
                length: 1
            },
        ]
    );
    store
        .remove_parked(&[(queue, id(2, 0)), (queue, id(3, 0))])
        .await?;
    assert_eq!(store.read_parked(&queue, None, 10).await?, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(
        store.load_parked_heads(0x70).await?,
        vec![ParkedHead {
            order_key: other,
            head: id(1, 0),
            length: 1
        }]
    );
    store
        .append_parked(&[event(queue, id(4, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying)])
        .await?;
    let ids: Vec<EventId> = store
        .read_parked(&queue, None, 10)
        .await?
        .iter()
        .map(|event| event.id)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(ids, vec![id(4, 0)]);
    Ok(())
}

async fn the_leader_record_is_a_compare_and_swap<F, Fut>(
    store: &dyn StateStore,
    open: &F,
) -> TestResult
where
    F: Fn() -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = TestResult<Arc<dyn StateStore>>>,
{
    assert_eq!(store.read_leader().await?, None);
    let stale = Version("not-a-version".to_owned());
    let first = leader(1, "task-a", LeaderState::Active);
    assert_eq!(
        store.swap_leader(&first, Some(&stale)).await?,
        SwapOutcome::Conflict
    );
    let SwapOutcome::Swapped(v1) = store.swap_leader(&first, None).await? else {
        return Err("creating the leader record failed".into());
    };
    assert_eq!(
        store.read_leader().await?,
        Some(Versioned {
            value: first.clone(),
            version: v1.clone()
        })
    );
    let second = leader(2, "task-b", LeaderState::Active);
    assert_eq!(
        store.swap_leader(&second, None).await?,
        SwapOutcome::Conflict
    );
    assert_eq!(
        store.swap_leader(&second, Some(&stale)).await?,
        SwapOutcome::Conflict
    );
    let SwapOutcome::Swapped(v2) = store.swap_leader(&second, Some(&v1)).await? else {
        return Err("swapping with the current version failed".into());
    };
    assert_ne!(v1, v2);
    let third = leader(3, "task-c", LeaderState::Released);
    assert_eq!(
        store.swap_leader(&third, Some(&v1)).await?,
        SwapOutcome::Conflict
    );
    assert_eq!(
        open().await?.read_leader().await?,
        Some(Versioned {
            value: second,
            version: v2
        })
    );
    Ok(())
}

async fn concurrent_calls_are_safe(store: &Arc<dyn StateStore>) -> TestResult {
    let queue = key(0x80, 1)?;
    let mut appends = tokio::task::JoinSet::new();
    for task in 0..16_u32 {
        let store = Arc::clone(store);
        appends.spawn(async move {
            let mut events: Vec<ParkedEvent> = (0..24)
                .map(|n| event(queue, id(100, n), ParkStatus::Retrying))
                .collect();
            events.rotate_left(usize::try_from(task)?);
            store.append_parked(&events).await?;
            TestResult::Ok(())
        });
    }
    while let Some(joined) = appends.join_next().await {
        joined??;
    }
    let ids: Vec<EventId> = store
        .read_parked(&queue, None, 100)
        .await?
        .iter()
        .map(|event| event.id)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(ids, (0..24).map(|n| id(100, n)).collect::<Vec<_>>());

    let Some(current) = store.read_leader().await? else {
        return Err("the leader record is missing".into());
    };
    let mut swaps = tokio::task::JoinSet::new();
    for task in 0..8_u64 {
        let store = Arc::clone(store);
        let expected = current.version.clone();
        swaps.spawn(async move {
            let next = leader(10 + task, "racer", LeaderState::Active);
            store.swap_leader(&next, Some(&expected)).await
        });
    }
    let mut swapped = 0;
    while let Some(joined) = swaps.join_next().await {
        if matches!(joined??, SwapOutcome::Swapped(_)) {
            swapped += 1;
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(swapped, 1, "exactly one concurrent swap must win");
    Ok(())
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/state/mod.rs` (new file):

```rust
pub mod filesystem;
pub mod memory;
pub mod s3;

use std::fmt;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

use async_trait::async_trait;
use bytes::Bytes;
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_decoder::lsn::ParseLsnError;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::value::RawValue;

use crate::event::EventId;
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;
use crate::sink::{AttributeValue, Attributes, Outbound};

pub const PARTITIONS: usize = 256;
pub const LEADER_OBJECT: &str = "leader.json";
pub const PARKED_DIR: &str = "parked";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum ParkStatus {
    Retrying,
    Dead,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ParkedEvent {
    pub order_key: OrderKeyId,
    pub id: EventId,
    pub status: ParkStatus,
    pub attempts: u32,
    pub first_failure_ms: u64,
    pub last_error: String,
    pub attributes: Attributes,
    pub body: Bytes,
}

impl ParkedEvent {
    pub fn outbound(&self) -> Outbound {
        Outbound {
            id: self.id,
            order_key: self.order_key,
            attributes: self.attributes.clone(),
            body: self.body.clone(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ParkedHead {
    pub order_key: OrderKeyId,
    pub head: EventId,
    pub length: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum LeaderState {
    Active,
    Released,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(into = "StoredLeader", try_from = "StoredLeader")]
pub struct LeaderRecord {
    pub epoch: u64,
    pub holder: String,
    pub state: LeaderState,
    pub confirmed_lsn: Lsn,
    pub updated_ms: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub struct Version(pub String);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Versioned<T> {
    pub value: T,
    pub version: Version,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum SwapOutcome {
    Swapped(Version),
    Conflict,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum StoreError {
    #[error("state store unavailable: {0}")]
    Unavailable(String),
    #[error("{0} is not in the store")]
    NotFound(String),
    #[error("stored object {key} is malformed: {reason}")]
    Malformed { key: String, reason: String },
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait StateStore: Send + Sync + fmt::Debug {
    async fn append_parked(&self, events: &[ParkedEvent]) -> Result<(), StoreError>;
    async fn load_parked_heads(&self, partition: u8) -> Result<Vec<ParkedHead>, StoreError>;
    async fn read_parked(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        after: Option<&EventId>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ParkedEvent>, StoreError>;
    async fn set_parked_status(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        id: &EventId,
        status: ParkStatus,
    ) -> Result<(), StoreError>;
    async fn remove_parked(&self, events: &[(OrderKeyId, EventId)]) -> Result<(), StoreError>;
    async fn read_leader(&self) -> Result<Option<Versioned<LeaderRecord>>, StoreError>;
    async fn swap_leader(
        &self,
        next: &LeaderRecord,
        expected: Option<&Version>,
    ) -> Result<SwapOutcome, StoreError>;
}

pub fn store_root(prefix: &str, pipeline: &str) -> String {
    let prefix = prefix.trim_matches('/');
    if prefix.is_empty() {
        pipeline.to_owned()
    } else {
        format!("{prefix}/{pipeline}")
    }
}

pub fn parked_name(key: &OrderKeyId, id: &EventId) -> String {
    format!("{key}/{id}.json")
}

pub fn parse_parked_name(name: &str) -> Option<(OrderKeyId, EventId)> {
    let (key, file) = name.split_once('/')?;
    let id = file.strip_suffix(".json")?;
    Some((key.parse().ok()?, id.parse().ok()?))
}

pub fn now_ms() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map_or(0, |elapsed| {
            u64::try_from(elapsed.as_millis()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX)
        })
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct StoredAttribute {
    name: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    data_type: String,
    value: String,
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct StoredParkedRef<'a> {
    order_key: String,
    id: String,
    status: ParkStatus,
    attempts: u32,
    first_failure_ms: u64,
    last_error: &'a str,
    attributes: Vec<StoredAttribute>,
    envelope: &'a RawValue,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct StoredParked {
    order_key: String,
    id: String,
    status: ParkStatus,
    attempts: u32,
    first_failure_ms: u64,
    last_error: String,
    attributes: Vec<StoredAttribute>,
    envelope: Box<RawValue>,
}

pub fn encode_parked(event: &ParkedEvent) -> Result<Vec<u8>, StoreError> {
    let malformed = |reason: String| StoreError::Malformed {
        key: parked_name(&event.order_key, &event.id),
        reason,
    };
    let envelope: &RawValue = serde_json::from_slice(&event.body)
        .map_err(|error| malformed(format!("the envelope is not JSON: {error}")))?;
    let stored = StoredParkedRef {
        order_key: event.order_key.to_string(),
        id: event.id.to_string(),
        status: event.status,
        attempts: event.attempts,
        first_failure_ms: event.first_failure_ms,
        last_error: &event.last_error,
        attributes: event
            .attributes
            .iter()
            .map(|(name, value)| StoredAttribute {
                name: name.clone(),
                data_type: value.data_type().to_owned(),
                value: value.as_str().to_owned(),
            })
            .collect(),
        envelope,
    };
    serde_json::to_vec(&stored).map_err(|error| malformed(error.to_string()))
}

pub fn decode_parked(name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<ParkedEvent, StoreError> {
    let malformed = |reason: String| StoreError::Malformed {
        key: name.to_owned(),
        reason,
    };
    let stored: StoredParked =
        serde_json::from_slice(bytes).map_err(|error| malformed(error.to_string()))?;
    let mut attributes = Vec::with_capacity(stored.attributes.len());
    for attribute in stored.attributes {
        let value = match attribute.data_type.as_str() {
            "String" => AttributeValue::String(attribute.value),
            "Number" => AttributeValue::Number(attribute.value),
            other => return Err(malformed(format!("unknown attribute type {other:?}"))),
        };
        attributes.push((attribute.name, value));
    }
    Ok(ParkedEvent {
        order_key: stored
            .order_key
            .parse()
            .map_err(|error| malformed(format!("{error}")))?,
        id: stored
            .id
            .parse()
            .map_err(|error| malformed(format!("{error}")))?,
        status: stored.status,
        attempts: stored.attempts,
        first_failure_ms: stored.first_failure_ms,
        last_error: stored.last_error,
        attributes,
        body: Bytes::from(stored.envelope.get().to_owned()),
    })
}

pub fn decode_leader(name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<LeaderRecord, StoreError> {
    serde_json::from_slice(bytes).map_err(|error| StoreError::Malformed {
        key: name.to_owned(),
        reason: error.to_string(),
    })
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct StoredLeader {
    epoch: u64,
    holder: String,
    state: LeaderState,
    confirmed_lsn: String,
    updated_ms: u64,
}

impl From<LeaderRecord> for StoredLeader {
    fn from(record: LeaderRecord) -> Self {
        Self {
            epoch: record.epoch,
            holder: record.holder,
            state: record.state,
            confirmed_lsn: record.confirmed_lsn.to_string(),
            updated_ms: record.updated_ms,
        }
    }
}

impl TryFrom<StoredLeader> for LeaderRecord {
    type Error = ParseLsnError;

    fn try_from(stored: StoredLeader) -> Result<Self, Self::Error> {
        Ok(Self {
            epoch: stored.epoch,
            holder: stored.holder,
            state: stored.state,
            confirmed_lsn: stored.confirmed_lsn.parse()?,
            updated_ms: stored.updated_ms,
        })
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn event() -> ParkedEvent {
        ParkedEvent {
            order_key: "ab000000000000000000000000000001".parse().unwrap(),
            id: EventId::new(Lsn(0x1A2B_3C4D), 3),
            status: ParkStatus::Dead,
            attempts: 7,
            first_failure_ms: 1_760_443_200_123,
            last_error: "InvalidParameter: \"bad\"".to_owned(),
            attributes: vec![
                (
                    "table".to_owned(),
                    AttributeValue::String("orders".to_owned()),
                ),
                (
                    "amount".to_owned(),
                    AttributeValue::Number("12.50".to_owned()),
                ),
            ],
            body: Bytes::from_static(
                r#"{"id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000003","after":{"n":12345678901234567890.10,"t":"café \"x\""}}"#
                    .as_bytes(),
            ),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn parked_events_round_trip_through_the_codec_byte_for_byte() {
        let event = event();
        let encoded = encode_parked(&event).unwrap();
        let text = String::from_utf8(encoded.clone()).unwrap();
        assert!(
            text.starts_with(r#"{"order_key":"ab000000000000000000000000000001","id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000003","status":"dead","attempts":7,"#),
            "{text}"
        );
        assert!(
            text.ends_with(r#""envelope":{"id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000003","after":{"n":12345678901234567890.10,"t":"café \"x\""}}}"#),
            "{text}"
        );
        assert_eq!(decode_parked("x", &encoded).unwrap(), event);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_body_that_is_not_json_cannot_be_encoded() {
        let mut event = event();
        event.body = Bytes::from_static(b"not json");
        assert!(matches!(
            encode_parked(&event),
            Err(StoreError::Malformed { key, .. }) if key == "ab000000000000000000000000000001/000000001a2b3c4d-00000003.json"
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn malformed_objects_are_reported_with_their_name() {
        for bytes in [
            &b"{"[..],
            br#"{"order_key":"zz","id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000003","status":"dead","attempts":1,"first_failure_ms":1,"last_error":"","attributes":[],"envelope":{}}"#,
            br#"{"order_key":"ab000000000000000000000000000001","id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000003","status":"dead","attempts":1,"first_failure_ms":1,"last_error":"","attributes":[{"name":"a","type":"Binary","value":""}],"envelope":{}}"#,
        ] {
            assert!(
                matches!(decode_parked("p/q.json", bytes), Err(StoreError::Malformed { key, .. }) if key == "p/q.json"),
                "{}",
                String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes)
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn leader_records_keep_the_lsn_in_postgres_notation() {
        let record = LeaderRecord {
            epoch: 4,
            holder: "task-a".to_owned(),
            state: LeaderState::Released,
            confirmed_lsn: Lsn(0x1_0000_00FF),
            updated_ms: 9,
        };
        let text = serde_json::to_string(&record).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            text,
            r#"{"epoch":4,"holder":"task-a","state":"released","confirmed_lsn":"1/FF","updated_ms":9}"#
        );
        assert_eq!(decode_leader("leader.json", text.as_bytes()), Ok(record));
        assert!(matches!(
            decode_leader(
                "leader.json",
                br#"{"epoch":4,"holder":"a","state":"active","confirmed_lsn":"x","updated_ms":9}"#
            ),
            Err(StoreError::Malformed { .. })
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn object_names_follow_the_spec_layout() {
        assert_eq!(store_root("orders/", "orders"), "orders/orders");
        assert_eq!(store_root("/a/b/", "p"), "a/b/p");
        assert_eq!(store_root("", "p"), "p");
        let event = event();
        let name = parked_name(&event.order_key, &event.id);
        assert_eq!(
            name,
            "ab000000000000000000000000000001/000000001a2b3c4d-00000003.json"
        );
        assert_eq!(parse_parked_name(&name), Some((event.order_key, event.id)));
        for bad in [
            "ab000000000000000000000000000001/000000001a2b3c4d-00000003",
            "ab000000000000000000000000000001/.tmp-1",
            "ab00000000000000000000000000001/000000001a2b3c4d-00000003.json",
            "ab000000000000000000000000000001",
        ] {
            assert_eq!(parse_parked_name(bad), None, "{bad}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_parked_event_rebuilds_its_outbound_message() {
        let event = event();
        let outbound = event.outbound();
        assert_eq!(outbound.id, event.id);
        assert_eq!(outbound.order_key, event.order_key);
        assert_eq!(outbound.attributes, event.attributes);
        assert_eq!(outbound.body, event.body);
    }
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/state/memory.rs` (new file):

```rust
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, MutexGuard};

use async_trait::async_trait;

use super::{
    LeaderRecord, ParkStatus, ParkedEvent, ParkedHead, StateStore, StoreError, SwapOutcome,
    Version, Versioned,
};
use crate::event::EventId;
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct MemoryStateStore {
    inner: Arc<Mutex<Inner>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct Inner {
    parked: BTreeMap<OrderKeyId, BTreeMap<EventId, ParkedEvent>>,
    leader: Option<(LeaderRecord, u64)>,
}

impl MemoryStateStore {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn parked_events(&self) -> usize {
        self.lock()
            .map_or(0, |inner| inner.parked.values().map(BTreeMap::len).sum())
    }

    fn lock(&self) -> Result<MutexGuard<'_, Inner>, StoreError> {
        self.inner
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| StoreError::Unavailable("the memory store is poisoned".to_owned()))
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl StateStore for MemoryStateStore {
    async fn append_parked(&self, events: &[ParkedEvent]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let mut inner = self.lock()?;
        for event in events {
            inner
                .parked
                .entry(event.order_key)
                .or_default()
                .entry(event.id)
                .or_insert_with(|| event.clone());
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn load_parked_heads(&self, partition: u8) -> Result<Vec<ParkedHead>, StoreError> {
        let inner = self.lock()?;
        Ok(inner
            .parked
            .iter()
            .filter(|(key, _)| key.partition() == partition)
            .filter_map(|(key, queue)| {
                let head = *queue.keys().next()?;
                Some(ParkedHead {
                    order_key: *key,
                    head,
                    length: u64::try_from(queue.len()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
                })
            })
            .collect())
    }

    async fn read_parked(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        after: Option<&EventId>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ParkedEvent>, StoreError> {
        let inner = self.lock()?;
        let Some(queue) = inner.parked.get(key) else {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        };
        Ok(queue
            .values()
            .filter(|event| after.is_none_or(|after| event.id > *after))
            .take(limit)
            .cloned()
            .collect())
    }

    async fn set_parked_status(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        id: &EventId,
        status: ParkStatus,
    ) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let mut inner = self.lock()?;
        let event = inner
            .parked
            .get_mut(key)
            .and_then(|queue| queue.get_mut(id))
            .ok_or_else(|| StoreError::NotFound(super::parked_name(key, id)))?;
        event.status = status;
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn remove_parked(&self, events: &[(OrderKeyId, EventId)]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let mut inner = self.lock()?;
        for (key, id) in events {
            if let Some(queue) = inner.parked.get_mut(key) {
                queue.remove(id);
                if queue.is_empty() {
                    inner.parked.remove(key);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn read_leader(&self) -> Result<Option<Versioned<LeaderRecord>>, StoreError> {
        let inner = self.lock()?;
        Ok(inner.leader.as_ref().map(|(record, version)| Versioned {
            value: record.clone(),
            version: Version(version.to_string()),
        }))
    }

    async fn swap_leader(
        &self,
        next: &LeaderRecord,
        expected: Option<&Version>,
    ) -> Result<SwapOutcome, StoreError> {
        let mut inner = self.lock()?;
        let current = inner.leader.as_ref().map(|(_, version)| *version);
        let matches = match (current, expected) {
            (None, None) => true,
            (Some(current), Some(expected)) => expected.0 == current.to_string(),
            _ => false,
        };
        if !matches {
            return Ok(SwapOutcome::Conflict);
        }
        let version = current.map_or(1, |current| current + 1);
        inner.leader = Some((next.clone(), version));
        Ok(SwapOutcome::Swapped(Version(version.to_string())))
    }
}
```

Create `crates/pg-cdc/src/state/filesystem.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/state/s3.rs` and `crates/pg-cdc/src/payload.rs` as empty files.

### Task 2: Filesystem state store

**Depends on:** Task 1
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Implements `FilesystemStateStore` (spec §11.5, R66): the §11.4 tree under `state_store.path`, create-only appends through a synced temp file and `hard_link`, directory fsyncs for every new entry, status changes by `rename`, removal of emptied queue directories, and the leader compare-and-swap under an exclusive `std::fs::File::lock` on `leader.lock` with the version kept in the record file. All blocking IO runs in `spawn_blocking` behind a semaphore of `max_concurrency`. Single host only (§11.5).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/state/filesystem.rs` (empty from Task 1)
- Test: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/state_filesystem.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything `pg_cdc::state` exports (Task 1), the suite in `tests/support/state_store.rs`.
- Produces: `pg_cdc::state::filesystem::{FilesystemStateStore, LEADER_LOCK}`; `FilesystemStateStore::new(path: &Path, pipeline: &str, max_concurrency: usize) -> Self` (`Clone`, `Debug`); the store's root is `path.join(pipeline)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Besides the conformance suite, the tests pin the on-disk layout, that temp files, stray files and non-key directories are ignored, that an emptied queue leaves no directory, and that an unwritable root reports `Unavailable` for writes and reads.

`crates/pg-cdc/tests/state_filesystem.rs` (new file):

```rust
#[path = "support/state_store.rs"]
mod state_store;

use std::sync::Arc;

use pg_cdc::state::filesystem::FilesystemStateStore;
use pg_cdc::state::{ParkStatus, StateStore, StoreError, decode_parked};

use state_store::{TestResult, event, id, key};

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_filesystem_store_conforms() -> TestResult {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
    let path = dir.path().to_owned();
    state_store::conforms(|| {
        let store: Arc<dyn StateStore> = Arc::new(FilesystemStateStore::new(&path, "orders", 4));
        async move { Ok(store) }
    })
    .await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn parked_events_use_the_spec_layout_and_stray_files_are_ignored() -> TestResult {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
    let store = FilesystemStateStore::new(dir.path(), "orders", 4);
    let queue = key(0xab, 1)?;
    let parked = event(queue, id(0x1A2B_3C4D, 3), ParkStatus::Retrying);
    store.append_parked(std::slice::from_ref(&parked)).await?;
    let queue_dir = dir
        .path()
        .join("orders")
        .join("parked")
        .join(queue.to_string());
    let object = queue_dir.join("000000001a2b3c4d-00000003.json");
    let name = format!("{queue}/000000001a2b3c4d-00000003.json");
    assert_eq!(decode_parked(&name, &std::fs::read(&object)?)?, parked);
    std::fs::write(
        queue_dir.join(".tmp-000000001a2b3c4d-00000004.json-1"),
        b"partial",
    )?;
    std::fs::write(queue_dir.join("notes.txt"), b"x")?;
    std::fs::create_dir_all(
        dir.path()
            .join("orders")
            .join("parked")
            .join("ab-not-a-key"),
    )?;
    let heads = store.load_parked_heads(0xab).await?;
    assert_eq!(heads.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(heads[0].length, 1);
    assert_eq!(
        store.read_parked(&queue, None, 10).await?,
        vec![parked.clone()]
    );
    std::fs::remove_file(queue_dir.join(".tmp-000000001a2b3c4d-00000004.json-1"))?;
    std::fs::remove_file(queue_dir.join("notes.txt"))?;
    store.remove_parked(&[(queue, parked.id)]).await?;
    assert!(!queue_dir.exists(), "an emptied queue leaves no directory");
    let leader_dir = dir.path().join("orders");
    assert!(!leader_dir.join("leader.json").exists());
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_unwritable_root_reports_the_store_unavailable() -> TestResult {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
    let blocker = dir.path().join("file");
    std::fs::write(&blocker, b"not a directory")?;
    let store = FilesystemStateStore::new(&blocker, "orders", 4);
    let queue = key(0x01, 1)?;
    let result = store
        .append_parked(&[event(queue, id(1, 0), ParkStatus::Retrying)])
        .await;
    assert!(
        matches!(result, Err(StoreError::Unavailable(_))),
        "{result:?}"
    );
    assert!(matches!(
        store.read_leader().await,
        Err(StoreError::Unavailable(_))
    ));
    Ok(())
}
```
