- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`crates/pg-cdc/src/state/filesystem.rs` (replace the empty file with):

```rust
use std::fs::{self, File, OpenOptions};
use std::io::{self, ErrorKind, Write as _};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use tokio::sync::Semaphore;

use super::{
    LEADER_OBJECT, LeaderRecord, PARKED_DIR, ParkStatus, ParkedEvent, ParkedHead, StateStore,
    StoreError, SwapOutcome, Version, Versioned, decode_parked, encode_parked,
};
use crate::event::EventId;
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;

pub const LEADER_LOCK: &str = "leader.lock";
const CREATE_ATTEMPTS: usize = 3;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct FilesystemStateStore {
    root: PathBuf,
    permits: Arc<Semaphore>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct LeaderFile {
    version: u64,
    record: LeaderRecord,
}

impl FilesystemStateStore {
    pub fn new(path: &Path, pipeline: &str, max_concurrency: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            root: path.join(pipeline),
            permits: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(max_concurrency.max(1))),
        }
    }

    async fn blocking<T, F>(&self, operation: F) -> Result<T, StoreError>
    where
        T: Send + 'static,
        F: FnOnce(&Path) -> Result<T, StoreError> + Send + 'static,
    {
        let _permit = self
            .permits
            .acquire()
            .await
            .map_err(|_| StoreError::Unavailable("the store is closed".to_owned()))?;
        let root = self.root.clone();
        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || operation(&root))
            .await
            .map_err(|error| StoreError::Unavailable(error.to_string()))?
    }
}

fn unavailable(path: &Path, error: &io::Error) -> StoreError {
    StoreError::Unavailable(format!("{}: {error}", path.display()))
}

fn sync_dir(dir: &Path) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    File::open(dir)
        .and_then(|handle| handle.sync_all())
        .map_err(|error| unavailable(dir, &error))
}

fn write_synced(path: &Path, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    let mut file = OpenOptions::new()
        .write(true)
        .create_new(true)
        .open(path)
        .map_err(|error| unavailable(path, &error))?;
    file.write_all(bytes)
        .and_then(|()| file.sync_all())
        .map_err(|error| unavailable(path, &error))
}

fn temp_path(dir: &Path, name: &str) -> PathBuf {
    dir.join(format!(".tmp-{name}-{:016x}", fastrand::u64(..)))
}

fn ensure_dir(root: &Path, dir: &Path) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    if dir.is_dir() {
        return Ok(());
    }
    fs::create_dir_all(dir).map_err(|error| unavailable(dir, &error))?;
    for ancestor in dir.ancestors().skip(1) {
        sync_dir(ancestor)?;
        if !ancestor.starts_with(root) {
            break;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn create_once(root: &Path, dir: &Path, name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    let mut last = None;
    for _ in 0..CREATE_ATTEMPTS {
        ensure_dir(root, dir)?;
        let temp = temp_path(dir, name);
        match write_synced(&temp, bytes) {
            Ok(()) => {}
            Err(error) if !dir.is_dir() => {
                last = Some(error);
                continue;
            }
            Err(error) => return Err(error),
        }
        let target = dir.join(name);
        let linked = fs::hard_link(&temp, &target);
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp);
        return match linked {
            Ok(()) => sync_dir(dir),
            Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::AlreadyExists => Ok(()),
            Err(error) => Err(unavailable(&target, &error)),
        };
    }
    Err(last.unwrap_or_else(|| StoreError::Unavailable(format!("{} vanished", dir.display()))))
}

fn replace(dir: &Path, name: &str, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    let temp = temp_path(dir, name);
    write_synced(&temp, bytes)?;
    let target = dir.join(name);
    if let Err(error) = fs::rename(&temp, &target) {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp);
        return Err(unavailable(&target, &error));
    }
    sync_dir(dir)
}

fn event_ids(dir: &Path) -> Result<Vec<EventId>, StoreError> {
    let entries = match fs::read_dir(dir) {
        Ok(entries) => entries,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(Vec::new()),
        Err(error) => return Err(unavailable(dir, &error)),
    };
    let mut ids = Vec::new();
    for entry in entries {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| unavailable(dir, &error))?;
        let name = entry.file_name();
        let Some(id) = name
            .to_str()
            .and_then(|name| name.strip_suffix(".json"))
            .and_then(|stem| stem.parse::<EventId>().ok())
        else {
            continue;
        };
        ids.push(id);
    }
    ids.sort_unstable();
    Ok(ids)
}

fn key_dir(root: &Path, key: &OrderKeyId) -> PathBuf {
    root.join(PARKED_DIR).join(key.to_string())
}

fn append(root: &Path, events: &[(OrderKeyId, String, Vec<u8>)]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    for (key, name, bytes) in events {
        create_once(root, &key_dir(root, key), name, bytes)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn load_heads(root: &Path, partition: u8) -> Result<Vec<ParkedHead>, StoreError> {
    let parked = root.join(PARKED_DIR);
    let entries = match fs::read_dir(&parked) {
        Ok(entries) => entries,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(Vec::new()),
        Err(error) => return Err(unavailable(&parked, &error)),
    };
    let mut heads = Vec::new();
    for entry in entries {
        let entry = entry.map_err(|error| unavailable(&parked, &error))?;
        let Some(order_key) = entry
            .file_name()
            .to_str()
            .and_then(|name| name.parse::<OrderKeyId>().ok())
        else {
            continue;
        };
        if order_key.partition() != partition {
            continue;
        }
        let ids = event_ids(&entry.path())?;
        if let Some(head) = ids.first() {
            heads.push(ParkedHead {
                order_key,
                head: *head,
                length: u64::try_from(ids.len()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
            });
        }
    }
    Ok(heads)
}

fn read(
    root: &Path,
    key: OrderKeyId,
    after: Option<EventId>,
    limit: usize,
) -> Result<Vec<ParkedEvent>, StoreError> {
    let dir = key_dir(root, &key);
    let mut events = Vec::new();
    for id in event_ids(&dir)? {
        if events.len() >= limit {
            break;
        }
        if after.is_some_and(|after| id <= after) {
            continue;
        }
        let name = format!("{id}.json");
        let path = dir.join(&name);
        let bytes = match fs::read(&path) {
            Ok(bytes) => bytes,
            Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => continue,
            Err(error) => return Err(unavailable(&path, &error)),
        };
        events.push(decode_parked(&super::parked_name(&key, &id), &bytes)?);
    }
    Ok(events)
}

fn set_status(
    root: &Path,
    key: OrderKeyId,
    id: EventId,
    status: ParkStatus,
) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    let dir = key_dir(root, &key);
    let name = format!("{id}.json");
    let path = dir.join(&name);
    let object = super::parked_name(&key, &id);
    let bytes = match fs::read(&path) {
        Ok(bytes) => bytes,
        Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            return Err(StoreError::NotFound(object));
        }
        Err(error) => return Err(unavailable(&path, &error)),
    };
    let mut event = decode_parked(&object, &bytes)?;
    event.status = status;
    replace(&dir, &name, &encode_parked(&event)?)
}

fn remove(root: &Path, events: &[(OrderKeyId, EventId)]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
    let parked = root.join(PARKED_DIR);
    for (key, id) in events {
        let dir = key_dir(root, key);
        let path = dir.join(format!("{id}.json"));
        match fs::remove_file(&path) {
            Ok(()) => sync_dir(&dir)?,
            Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => continue,
            Err(error) => return Err(unavailable(&path, &error)),
        }
        if fs::remove_dir(&dir).is_ok() {
            sync_dir(&parked)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn read_leader_file(root: &Path) -> Result<Option<LeaderFile>, StoreError> {
    let path = root.join(LEADER_OBJECT);
    match fs::read(&path) {
        Ok(bytes) => {
            serde_json::from_slice(&bytes)
                .map(Some)
                .map_err(|error| StoreError::Malformed {
                    key: LEADER_OBJECT.to_owned(),
                    reason: error.to_string(),
                })
        }
        Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(error) => Err(unavailable(&path, &error)),
    }
}

fn swap(
    root: &Path,
    next: LeaderRecord,
    expected: Option<&Version>,
) -> Result<SwapOutcome, StoreError> {
    ensure_dir(root, root)?;
    let lock_path = root.join(LEADER_LOCK);
    let lock = OpenOptions::new()
        .create(true)
        .truncate(false)
        .write(true)
        .open(&lock_path)
        .map_err(|error| unavailable(&lock_path, &error))?;
    lock.lock()
        .map_err(|error| unavailable(&lock_path, &error))?;
    let current = read_leader_file(root)?.map(|file| file.version);
    let matches = match (current, expected) {
        (None, None) => true,
        (Some(current), Some(expected)) => expected.0 == current.to_string(),
        _ => false,
    };
    if !matches {
        return Ok(SwapOutcome::Conflict);
    }
    let version = current.map_or(1, |current| current.saturating_add(1));
    let file = LeaderFile {
        version,
        record: next,
    };
    let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&file).map_err(|error| StoreError::Malformed {
        key: LEADER_OBJECT.to_owned(),
        reason: error.to_string(),
    })?;
    replace(root, LEADER_OBJECT, &bytes)?;
    Ok(SwapOutcome::Swapped(Version(version.to_string())))
}

#[async_trait]
impl StateStore for FilesystemStateStore {
    async fn append_parked(&self, events: &[ParkedEvent]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        if events.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let mut encoded = Vec::with_capacity(events.len());
        for event in events {
            encoded.push((
                event.order_key,
                format!("{}.json", event.id),
                encode_parked(event)?,
            ));
        }
        self.blocking(move |root| append(root, &encoded)).await
    }

    async fn load_parked_heads(&self, partition: u8) -> Result<Vec<ParkedHead>, StoreError> {
        self.blocking(move |root| load_heads(root, partition)).await
    }

    async fn read_parked(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        after: Option<&EventId>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ParkedEvent>, StoreError> {
        let (key, after) = (*key, after.copied());
        self.blocking(move |root| read(root, key, after, limit))
            .await
    }

    async fn set_parked_status(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        id: &EventId,
        status: ParkStatus,
    ) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let (key, id) = (*key, *id);
        self.blocking(move |root| set_status(root, key, id, status))
            .await
    }

    async fn remove_parked(&self, events: &[(OrderKeyId, EventId)]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        if events.is_empty() {
            return Ok(());
        }
        let events = events.to_vec();
        self.blocking(move |root| remove(root, &events)).await
    }

    async fn read_leader(&self) -> Result<Option<Versioned<LeaderRecord>>, StoreError> {
        self.blocking(|root| {
            Ok(read_leader_file(root)?.map(|file| Versioned {
                value: file.record,
                version: Version(file.version.to_string()),
            }))
        })
        .await
    }

    async fn swap_leader(
        &self,
        next: &LeaderRecord,
        expected: Option<&Version>,
    ) -> Result<SwapOutcome, StoreError> {
        let (next, expected) = (next.clone(), expected.cloned());
        self.blocking(move |root| swap(root, next, expected.as_ref()))
            .await
    }
}
```

### Task 3: S3 state store

**Depends on:** Task 1
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Implements `S3StateStore` (spec §11.4) and `s3_client`, the rustls S3 client with standard retries (R57). Parked events are written with `If-None-Match: *`; heads load by listing `parked/<pp>` page by page; `read_parked` lists with `StartAfter` and fetches the listed objects concurrently; status changes rewrite the object; removals delete each object; the leader record uses `If-Match` and `If-None-Match` (R65). Every request, list pages included, holds a permit from a semaphore of `max_concurrency`, and fan-outs (appends, reads, removals) spawn one task per object that waits for a permit, so a call with 10,000 objects never has more than `max_concurrency` requests in flight (the user asked for this bound explicitly). The test suite runs the conformance suite on moto, pins the object layout and the leader ETag, pages through more than 1,000 objects, distinguishes a missing bucket from an empty store, and measures the request concurrency against a slow local HTTP endpoint.

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

```rust
#[path = "support/state_store.rs"]
mod state_store;

use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::time::Duration;

use aws_sdk_s3::config::{BehaviorVersion, Credentials, Region};
use aws_smithy_http_client::tls::{Provider, rustls_provider::CryptoMode};
use pg_cdc::state::s3::S3StateStore;
use pg_cdc::state::{
    LeaderRecord, LeaderState, ParkStatus, ParkedEvent, StateStore, StoreError, SwapOutcome,
};
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_testkit::AwsEmulator;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader};
use tokio::net::TcpListener;

use state_store::{TestResult, event, id, key};

const BUCKET: &str = "pgcdc-state";

async fn emulator_with_bucket() -> TestResult<(AwsEmulator, aws_sdk_s3::Client)> {
    let aws = AwsEmulator::start().await?;
    let s3 = aws.s3().await;
    s3.create_bucket().bucket(BUCKET).send().await?;
    Ok((aws, s3))
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_s3_store_conforms() -> TestResult {
    let (_aws, s3) = emulator_with_bucket().await?;
    state_store::conforms(|| {
        let store: Arc<dyn StateStore> = Arc::new(S3StateStore::new(
            s3.clone(),
            BUCKET.to_owned(),
            "orders/",
            "orders",
            8,
        ));
        async move { Ok(store) }
    })
    .await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn parked_objects_and_the_leader_record_use_the_spec_layout() -> TestResult {
    let (_aws, s3) = emulator_with_bucket().await?;
    let store = S3StateStore::new(s3.clone(), BUCKET.to_owned(), "team/", "orders", 8);
    let queue = key(0xab, 1)?;
    store
        .append_parked(&[event(queue, id(0x1A2B_3C4D, 3), ParkStatus::Retrying)])
        .await?;
    let record = LeaderRecord {
        epoch: 1,
        holder: "task-a".to_owned(),
        state: LeaderState::Active,
        confirmed_lsn: Lsn(0x10),
        updated_ms: 1,
    };
    assert!(matches!(
        store.swap_leader(&record, None).await?,
        SwapOutcome::Swapped(_)
    ));
    let listed = s3.list_objects_v2().bucket(BUCKET).send().await?;
    let mut keys: Vec<&str> = listed
        .contents()
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|object| object.key())
        .collect();
    keys.sort_unstable();
    assert_eq!(
        keys,
        vec![
            "team/orders/leader.json".to_owned(),
            format!("team/orders/parked/{queue}/000000001a2b3c4d-00000003.json"),
        ]
    );
    let head = s3
        .head_object()
        .bucket(BUCKET)
        .key("team/orders/leader.json")
        .send()
        .await?;
    let Some(current) = store.read_leader().await? else {
        return Err("no leader record".into());
    };
    assert_eq!(head.e_tag(), Some(current.version.0.as_str()));
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn heads_load_across_listing_pages() -> TestResult {
    let (_aws, s3) = emulator_with_bucket().await?;
    let store = S3StateStore::new(s3, BUCKET.to_owned(), "", "orders", 32);
    let busy = key(0x42, 1)?;
    let quiet = key(0x42, 2)?;
    let mut events: Vec<ParkedEvent> = (0..1020)
        .map(|n| event(busy, id(7, n), ParkStatus::Retrying))
        .collect();
    events.extend((0..30).map(|n| event(quiet, id(9, n), ParkStatus::Retrying)));
    store.append_parked(&events).await?;
    let heads = store.load_parked_heads(0x42).await?;
    let summary: Vec<_> = heads
        .iter()
        .map(|head| (head.order_key, head.head, head.length))
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(summary, vec![(busy, id(7, 0), 1020), (quiet, id(9, 0), 30)]);
    let tail = store.read_parked(&busy, Some(&id(7, 1015)), 10).await?;
    let ids: Vec<_> = tail.iter().map(|event| event.id).collect();
    assert_eq!(ids, (1016..1020).map(|n| id(7, n)).collect::<Vec<_>>());
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_missing_bucket_reports_the_store_unavailable() -> TestResult {
    let aws = AwsEmulator::start().await?;
    let store = S3StateStore::new(aws.s3().await, "missing".to_owned(), "", "orders", 4);
    let result = store.load_parked_heads(0).await;
    assert!(
        matches!(&result, Err(StoreError::Unavailable(message)) if message.contains("NoSuchBucket")),
        "{result:?}"
    );
    let leader = store.read_leader().await;
    assert!(
        matches!(&leader, Err(StoreError::Unavailable(message)) if message.contains("NoSuchBucket")),
        "{leader:?}"
    );
    let removed = store.remove_parked(&[(key(0x01, 1)?, id(1, 0))]).await;
    assert!(
        matches!(removed, Err(StoreError::Unavailable(_))),
        "{removed:?}"
    );
    Ok(())
}

async fn serve_slowly(listener: TcpListener, active: Arc<AtomicUsize>, peak: Arc<AtomicUsize>) {
    while let Ok((socket, _)) = listener.accept().await {
        let (active, peak) = (Arc::clone(&active), Arc::clone(&peak));
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let (reader, mut writer) = socket.into_split();
            let mut reader = BufReader::new(reader);
            loop {
                let mut length = 0;
                let mut method = String::new();
                loop {
                    let mut line = String::new();
                    if reader.read_line(&mut line).await.unwrap_or(0) == 0 {
                        return;
                    }
                    if method.is_empty() {
                        method = line.split(' ').next().unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
                    }
                    if let Some(value) = line.to_ascii_lowercase().strip_prefix("content-length:") {
                        length = value.trim().parse().unwrap_or(0);
                    }
                    if line == "\r\n" {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                let mut body = vec![0; length];
                if reader.read_exact(&mut body).await.is_err() {
                    return;
                }
                let now = active.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;
                peak.fetch_max(now, Ordering::SeqCst);
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
                active.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
                let response = if method == "DELETE" {
                    "HTTP/1.1 204 No Content\r\n\r\n"
                } else {
                    "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nETag: \"e\"\r\nContent-Length: 0\r\n\r\n"
                };
                if writer.write_all(response.as_bytes()).await.is_err() {
                    return;
                }
            }
        });
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn s3_requests_never_exceed_max_concurrency() -> TestResult {
    let listener = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await?;
    let endpoint = format!("http://{}", listener.local_addr()?);
    let active = Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(0));
    let peak = Arc::new(AtomicUsize::new(0));
    tokio::spawn(serve_slowly(
        listener,
        Arc::clone(&active),
        Arc::clone(&peak),
    ));
    let http_client = aws_smithy_http_client::Builder::new()
        .tls_provider(Provider::Rustls(CryptoMode::Ring))
        .build_https();
    let config = aws_sdk_s3::Config::builder()
        .behavior_version(BehaviorVersion::latest())
        .region(Region::new("us-east-1"))
        .credentials_provider(Credentials::new("test", "test", None, None, "test"))
        .endpoint_url(endpoint)
        .force_path_style(true)
        .http_client(http_client)
        .build();
    let store = S3StateStore::new(
        aws_sdk_s3::Client::from_conf(config),
        BUCKET.to_owned(),
        "",
        "orders",
        4,
    );
    let queue = key(0x01, 1)?;
    let events: Vec<ParkedEvent> = (0..64)
        .map(|n| event(queue, id(1, n), ParkStatus::Retrying))
        .collect();
    store.append_parked(&events).await?;
    let removals: Vec<_> = events.iter().map(|event| (queue, event.id)).collect();
    store.remove_parked(&removals).await?;
    let peak = peak.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
    assert!(peak <= 4, "{peak} requests were in flight at once");
    assert!(peak >= 2, "the fan-out never ran in parallel");
    Ok(())
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/state/s3.rs` (replace the empty file with):

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use aws_config::retry::RetryConfig;
use aws_config::timeout::TimeoutConfig;
use aws_config::{BehaviorVersion, Region};
use aws_sdk_s3::Client;
use aws_sdk_s3::error::{DisplayErrorContext, ProvideErrorMetadata, SdkError};
use aws_sdk_s3::primitives::ByteStream;
use aws_smithy_http_client::tls::{Provider, rustls_provider::CryptoMode};
use tokio::sync::{Semaphore, SemaphorePermit};
use tokio::task::JoinSet;

use super::{
    LEADER_OBJECT, LeaderRecord, PARKED_DIR, ParkStatus, ParkedEvent, ParkedHead, StateStore,
    StoreError, SwapOutcome, Version, Versioned, decode_leader, decode_parked, encode_parked,
    parse_parked_name, store_root,
};
use crate::event::EventId;
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;

pub const S3_MAX_ATTEMPTS: u32 = 3;
pub const S3_ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);
pub const S3_OPERATION_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);
pub const S3_LIST_PAGE: i32 = 1000;
const JSON: &str = "application/json";

pub async fn s3_client(region: Option<String>) -> Client {
    let http_client = aws_smithy_http_client::Builder::new()
        .tls_provider(Provider::Rustls(CryptoMode::Ring))
        .build_https();
    let mut loader = aws_config::defaults(BehaviorVersion::latest())
        .http_client(http_client)
        .retry_config(RetryConfig::standard().with_max_attempts(S3_MAX_ATTEMPTS))
        .timeout_config(
            TimeoutConfig::builder()
                .operation_attempt_timeout(S3_ATTEMPT_TIMEOUT)
                .operation_timeout(S3_OPERATION_TIMEOUT)
                .build(),
        );
    if let Some(region) = region {
        loader = loader.region(Region::new(region));
    }
    Client::new(&loader.load().await)
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct S3StateStore {
    client: Client,
    bucket: String,
    root: String,
    permits: Arc<Semaphore>,
}

fn status<E>(error: &SdkError<E>) -> Option<u16> {
    error
        .raw_response()
        .map(|response| response.status().as_u16())
}

fn missing_key<E: ProvideErrorMetadata>(error: &SdkError<E>) -> bool {
    error
        .as_service_error()
        .and_then(ProvideErrorMetadata::code)
        == Some("NoSuchKey")
}

fn unavailable<E: std::error::Error + 'static>(error: &SdkError<E>) -> StoreError {
    StoreError::Unavailable(DisplayErrorContext(error).to_string())
}

impl S3StateStore {
    pub fn new(
        client: Client,
        bucket: String,
        prefix: &str,
        pipeline: &str,
        max_concurrency: usize,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            client,
            bucket,
            root: store_root(prefix, pipeline),
            permits: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(max_concurrency.max(1))),
        }
    }

    async fn permit(&self) -> Result<SemaphorePermit<'_>, StoreError> {
        self.permits
            .acquire()
            .await
            .map_err(|_| StoreError::Unavailable("the store is closed".to_owned()))
    }

    fn parked_prefix(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}/{PARKED_DIR}/", self.root)
    }

    fn object(&self, key: &OrderKeyId, id: &EventId) -> String {
        format!("{}{}", self.parked_prefix(), super::parked_name(key, id))
    }

    fn leader_object(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}/{LEADER_OBJECT}", self.root)
    }

    async fn put_new(&self, object: String, bytes: Vec<u8>) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let _permit = self.permit().await?;
        let sent = self
            .client
            .put_object()
            .bucket(&self.bucket)
            .key(&object)
            .content_type(JSON)
            .if_none_match("*")
            .body(ByteStream::from(bytes))
            .send()
            .await;
        match sent {
            Ok(_) => Ok(()),
            Err(error) if status(&error) == Some(412) => Ok(()),
            Err(error) => Err(unavailable(&error)),
        }
    }

    async fn get(&self, object: &str) -> Result<Option<(Vec<u8>, Option<String>)>, StoreError> {
        let _permit = self.permit().await?;
        let output = match self
            .client
            .get_object()
            .bucket(&self.bucket)
            .key(object)
            .send()
            .await
        {
            Ok(output) => output,
            Err(error) if missing_key(&error) => return Ok(None),
            Err(error) => return Err(unavailable(&error)),
        };
        let etag = output.e_tag().map(str::to_owned);
        let bytes = output
            .body
            .collect()
            .await
            .map_err(|error| StoreError::Unavailable(format!("{object}: {error}")))?
            .into_bytes()
            .to_vec();
        Ok(Some((bytes, etag)))
    }

    async fn delete(&self, object: String) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let _permit = self.permit().await?;
        match self
            .client
            .delete_object()
            .bucket(&self.bucket)
            .key(&object)
            .send()
            .await
        {
            Ok(_) => Ok(()),
            Err(error) => Err(unavailable(&error)),
        }
    }

    async fn list(
        &self,
        prefix: &str,
        start_after: Option<String>,
        token: Option<String>,
        max_keys: i32,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<String>, Option<String>), StoreError> {
        let _permit = self.permit().await?;
        let output = self
            .client
            .list_objects_v2()
            .bucket(&self.bucket)
            .prefix(prefix)
            .set_start_after(start_after)
            .set_continuation_token(token)
            .max_keys(max_keys)
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|error| unavailable(&error))?;
        let keys = output
            .contents()
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|object| object.key().map(str::to_owned))
            .collect();
        let next = output
            .is_truncated()
            .unwrap_or(false)
            .then(|| output.next_continuation_token().map(str::to_owned))
            .flatten();
        Ok((keys, next))
    }

    fn parse(&self, object: &str) -> Option<(OrderKeyId, EventId)> {
        parse_parked_name(object.strip_prefix(&self.parked_prefix())?)
    }

    async fn read_event(
        &self,
        key: OrderKeyId,
        id: EventId,
    ) -> Result<Option<ParkedEvent>, StoreError> {
        let object = self.object(&key, &id);
        let Some((bytes, _)) = self.get(&object).await? else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        decode_parked(&super::parked_name(&key, &id), &bytes).map(Some)
    }

    async fn join<T: Send + 'static>(
        mut tasks: JoinSet<Result<T, StoreError>>,
    ) -> Result<Vec<T>, StoreError> {
        let mut results = Vec::with_capacity(tasks.len());
        let mut failure = None;
        while let Some(joined) = tasks.join_next().await {
            match joined {
                Ok(Ok(value)) => results.push(value),
                Ok(Err(error)) => failure = Some(error),
                Err(error) => failure = Some(StoreError::Unavailable(error.to_string())),
            }
        }
        failure.map_or(Ok(results), Err)
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl StateStore for S3StateStore {
    async fn append_parked(&self, events: &[ParkedEvent]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let mut tasks = JoinSet::new();
        for event in events {
            let object = self.object(&event.order_key, &event.id);
            let bytes = encode_parked(event)?;
            let store = self.clone();
            tasks.spawn(async move { store.put_new(object, bytes).await });
        }
        Self::join(tasks).await.map(drop)
    }

    async fn load_parked_heads(&self, partition: u8) -> Result<Vec<ParkedHead>, StoreError> {
        let prefix = format!("{}{partition:02x}", self.parked_prefix());
        let mut heads: Vec<ParkedHead> = Vec::new();
        let mut token = None;
        loop {
            let (keys, next) = self.list(&prefix, None, token, S3_LIST_PAGE).await?;
            for (order_key, id) in keys.iter().filter_map(|object| self.parse(object)) {
                match heads.last_mut() {
                    Some(last) if last.order_key == order_key => {
                        last.length = last.length.saturating_add(1);
                    }
                    _ => heads.push(ParkedHead {
                        order_key,
                        head: id,
                        length: 1,
                    }),
                }
            }
            match next {
                Some(next) => token = Some(next),
                None => return Ok(heads),
            }
        }
    }

    async fn read_parked(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        after: Option<&EventId>,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<Vec<ParkedEvent>, StoreError> {
        let prefix = format!("{}{key}/", self.parked_prefix());
        let mut start_after = after.map(|after| self.object(key, after));
        let mut ids = Vec::new();
        while ids.len() < limit {
            let wanted = i32::try_from(limit - ids.len()).unwrap_or(S3_LIST_PAGE);
            let (objects, next) = self
                .list(&prefix, start_after.take(), None, wanted.min(S3_LIST_PAGE))
                .await?;
            let last = objects.last().cloned();
            ids.extend(
                objects
                    .iter()
                    .filter_map(|object| self.parse(object))
                    .map(|(_, id)| id),
            );
            match (next, last) {
                (Some(_), Some(last)) => start_after = Some(last),
                _ => break,
            }
        }
        ids.truncate(limit);
        let mut tasks = JoinSet::new();
        for id in ids {
            let (store, key) = (self.clone(), *key);
            tasks.spawn(async move { store.read_event(key, id).await });
        }
        let mut events: Vec<ParkedEvent> = Self::join(tasks).await?.into_iter().flatten().collect();
        events.sort_unstable_by_key(|event| event.id);
        Ok(events)
    }

    async fn set_parked_status(
        &self,
        key: &OrderKeyId,
        id: &EventId,
        status: ParkStatus,
    ) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let mut event = self
            .read_event(*key, *id)
            .await?
            .ok_or_else(|| StoreError::NotFound(super::parked_name(key, id)))?;
        event.status = status;
        let bytes = encode_parked(&event)?;
        let _permit = self.permit().await?;
        self.client
            .put_object()
            .bucket(&self.bucket)
            .key(self.object(key, id))
            .content_type(JSON)
            .body(ByteStream::from(bytes))
            .send()
            .await
            .map(drop)
            .map_err(|error| unavailable(&error))
    }

    async fn remove_parked(&self, events: &[(OrderKeyId, EventId)]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
        let mut tasks = JoinSet::new();
        for (key, id) in events {
            let (store, object) = (self.clone(), self.object(key, id));
            tasks.spawn(async move { store.delete(object).await });
        }
        Self::join(tasks).await.map(drop)
    }

    async fn read_leader(&self) -> Result<Option<Versioned<LeaderRecord>>, StoreError> {
        let Some((bytes, etag)) = self.get(&self.leader_object()).await? else {
            return Ok(None);
        };
        let version = etag.ok_or_else(|| StoreError::Malformed {
            key: LEADER_OBJECT.to_owned(),
            reason: "the object has no ETag".to_owned(),
        })?;
        Ok(Some(Versioned {
            value: decode_leader(LEADER_OBJECT, &bytes)?,
            version: Version(version),
        }))
    }

    async fn swap_leader(
        &self,
        next: &LeaderRecord,
        expected: Option<&Version>,
    ) -> Result<SwapOutcome, StoreError> {
        let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(next).map_err(|error| StoreError::Malformed {
            key: LEADER_OBJECT.to_owned(),
            reason: error.to_string(),
        })?;
        let request = self
            .client
            .put_object()
            .bucket(&self.bucket)
            .key(self.leader_object())
            .content_type(JSON)
            .body(ByteStream::from(bytes));
        let request = match expected {
            Some(version) => request.if_match(&version.0),
            None => request.if_none_match("*"),
        };
        let sent = {
            let _permit = self.permit().await?;
            request.send().await
        };
        match sent {
            Ok(output) => output
                .e_tag()
                .map(|etag| SwapOutcome::Swapped(Version(etag.to_owned())))
                .ok_or_else(|| StoreError::Malformed {
                    key: LEADER_OBJECT.to_owned(),
                    reason: "PutObject returned no ETag".to_owned(),
                }),
            Err(error) if status(&error) == Some(412) || missing_key(&error) => {
                match self.read_leader().await? {
                    Some(current) if current.value == *next => {
                        Ok(SwapOutcome::Swapped(current.version))
                    }
                    _ => Ok(SwapOutcome::Conflict),
                }
            }
            Err(error) => Err(unavailable(&error)),
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 4: Payload store and pointer events

**Depends on:** Task 1
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** high

Implements `pg_cdc::payload` (spec §11.6, §7.5, R62): the `PayloadStore` trait, `PayloadRef { uri, bytes }`, the S3 implementation writing `<prefix>/<pipeline>/payloads/<event id>.json` and returning an `s3://` URI, and `pointer_body`, which turns an encoded envelope into the pointer event: the same fields without `before`, `after` and `unchanged_toast`, with `payload_ref` before `source`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/Cargo.toml` and `Cargo.lock` when the task's worktree does not already have `aws-sdk-s3` (Task 3 runs in a parallel lane, so it usually does not): add `aws-sdk-s3.workspace = true` exactly as Task 3 does, regenerate `Cargo.lock` with `cargo metadata --format-version 1 > /dev/null`, and commit both; a later merge with Task 3 keeps the union (Global Constraints)
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/payload.rs` (empty from Task 1)
- Test: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/payload_s3.rs`; unit tests in `payload.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `pg_cdc::state::{StoreError, store_root}` (Task 1), `pg_cdc::event::EventId`, `pg_cdc::event::sample_envelope` (test-only).
- Produces: `pg_cdc::payload::{PayloadStore, PayloadRef, S3PayloadStore, pointer_body, PAYLOADS_DIR}`; `trait PayloadStore: Send + Sync + Debug { async fn put(&self, id: &EventId, body: &[u8]) -> Result<PayloadRef, StoreError>; }`; `struct PayloadRef { pub uri: String, pub bytes: u64 }` (`Serialize`); `S3PayloadStore::new(client: aws_sdk_s3::Client, bucket: String, prefix: &str, pipeline: &str)`; `pub fn pointer_body(body: &[u8], payload: &PayloadRef) -> Result<Bytes, serde_json::Error>`.

`crates/pg-cdc/tests/payload_s3.rs` (new file):

```rust
use pg_cdc::event::EventId;
use pg_cdc::payload::{PayloadRef, PayloadStore, S3PayloadStore};
use pg_cdc::state::StoreError;
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_testkit::AwsEmulator;

type TestResult<T = ()> = Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>>;

const BUCKET: &str = "pgcdc-payloads";

#[tokio::test]
async fn payloads_are_written_where_their_reference_points() -> TestResult {
    let aws = AwsEmulator::start().await?;
    let s3 = aws.s3().await;
    s3.create_bucket().bucket(BUCKET).send().await?;
    let store = S3PayloadStore::new(s3.clone(), BUCKET.to_owned(), "orders/", "orders");
    let id = EventId::new(Lsn(0x1A2B_3C4D), 7);
    let body = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{id}","after":{{"blob":"{}"}}}}"#,
        "x".repeat(300_000)
    );
    let reference = store.put(&id, body.as_bytes()).await?;
    assert_eq!(
        reference,
        PayloadRef {
            uri: "s3://pgcdc-payloads/orders/orders/payloads/000000001a2b3c4d-00000007.json"
                .to_owned(),
            bytes: u64::try_from(body.len())?,
        }
    );
    let replayed = store.put(&id, body.as_bytes()).await?;
    assert_eq!(replayed, reference);
    let object = s3
        .get_object()
        .bucket(BUCKET)
        .key("orders/orders/payloads/000000001a2b3c4d-00000007.json")
        .send()
        .await?;
    assert_eq!(object.content_type(), Some("application/json"));
    let stored = object.body.collect().await?.into_bytes();
    assert_eq!(stored.as_ref(), body.as_bytes());
    Ok(())
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_missing_bucket_reports_the_payload_store_unavailable() -> TestResult {
    let aws = AwsEmulator::start().await?;
    let store = S3PayloadStore::new(aws.s3().await, "missing".to_owned(), "", "orders");
    let result = store.put(&EventId::new(Lsn(1), 0), b"{}").await;
    assert!(
        matches!(&result, Err(StoreError::Unavailable(message)) if message.contains("NoSuchBucket")),
        "{result:?}"
    );
    Ok(())
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

The golden test pins the exact pointer text, field order included.

`crates/pg-cdc/src/payload.rs` (replace the empty file with):

```rust
use std::fmt;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use aws_sdk_s3::Client;
use aws_sdk_s3::error::DisplayErrorContext;
use aws_sdk_s3::primitives::ByteStream;
use bytes::Bytes;
use serde::Serialize;
use serde_json::{Map, Value};

use crate::event::EventId;
use crate::state::{StoreError, store_root};

pub const PAYLOADS_DIR: &str = "payloads";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize)]
pub struct PayloadRef {
    pub uri: String,
    pub bytes: u64,
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait PayloadStore: Send + Sync + fmt::Debug {
    async fn put(&self, id: &EventId, body: &[u8]) -> Result<PayloadRef, StoreError>;
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct S3PayloadStore {
    client: Client,
    bucket: String,
    root: String,
}

impl S3PayloadStore {
    pub fn new(client: Client, bucket: String, prefix: &str, pipeline: &str) -> Self {
        Self {
            client,
            bucket,
            root: store_root(prefix, pipeline),
        }
    }

    fn object(&self, id: &EventId) -> String {
        format!("{}/{PAYLOADS_DIR}/{id}.json", self.root)
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl PayloadStore for S3PayloadStore {
    async fn put(&self, id: &EventId, body: &[u8]) -> Result<PayloadRef, StoreError> {
        let object = self.object(id);
        self.client
            .put_object()
            .bucket(&self.bucket)
            .key(&object)
            .content_type("application/json")
            .body(ByteStream::from(body.to_vec()))
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|error| StoreError::Unavailable(DisplayErrorContext(&error).to_string()))?;
        Ok(PayloadRef {
            uri: format!("s3://{}/{object}", self.bucket),
            bytes: u64::try_from(body.len()).unwrap_or(u64::MAX),
        })
    }
}

pub fn pointer_body(body: &[u8], payload: &PayloadRef) -> Result<Bytes, serde_json::Error> {
    let envelope: Map<String, Value> = serde_json::from_slice(body)?;
    let mut reference = Some(serde_json::to_value(payload)?);
    let mut pointer = Map::with_capacity(envelope.len());
    for (name, value) in envelope {
        if matches!(name.as_str(), "before" | "after" | "unchanged_toast") {
            continue;
        }
        if name == "source"
            && let Some(reference) = reference.take()
        {
            pointer.insert("payload_ref".to_owned(), reference);
        }
        pointer.insert(name, value);
    }
    if let Some(reference) = reference {
        pointer.insert("payload_ref".to_owned(), reference);
    }
    serde_json::to_vec(&pointer).map(Bytes::from)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::event::{Op, sample_envelope};
    use serde_json::json;

    fn reference() -> PayloadRef {
        PayloadRef {
            uri: "s3://pgcdc-payloads/orders/orders/payloads/000000001a2b3c4d-00000000.json"
                .to_owned(),
            bytes: 912_345,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_pointer_is_the_envelope_without_row_images_plus_the_payload_ref() {
        let mut envelope = sample_envelope(
            Op::Update,
            Some(json!({"id": 42})),
            Some(json!({"id": 42, "status": "pending"})),
            Some(json!({"id": 42, "status": "paid"})),
        );
        envelope.unchanged_toast = vec!["doc".to_owned()];
        let body = envelope.encode().unwrap();
        let pointer = pointer_body(&body, &reference()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            std::str::from_utf8(&pointer).unwrap(),
            concat!(
                r#"{"id":"000000001a2b3c4d-00000000","version":1,"op":"update","schema":"public","table":"orders","key":{"id":42},"#,
                r#""payload_ref":{"uri":"s3://pgcdc-payloads/orders/orders/payloads/000000001a2b3c4d-00000000.json","bytes":912345},"#,
                r#""source":{"pipeline":"orders","commit_lsn":"0/1A2B3C4D","commit_ts":"2026-10-14T12:00:00.123456+00:00"}}"#
            )
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_pointer_keeps_exact_key_values() {
        let body = br#"{"id":"x","key":{"id":123456789012345678901234567890.000},"after":{"a":1},"source":{}}"#;
        let pointer = pointer_body(body, &reference()).unwrap();
        assert!(
            std::str::from_utf8(&pointer).unwrap().starts_with(
                r#"{"id":"x","key":{"id":123456789012345678901234567890.000},"payload_ref":"#
            ),
            "{pointer:?}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_body_that_is_not_an_object_has_no_pointer() {
        assert!(pointer_body(b"[1]", &reference()).is_err());
        assert!(pointer_body(b"not json", &reference()).is_err());
    }
}
```

### Task 5: Parking and store configuration

**Depends on:** none
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** normal

Adds the milestone 3 keys of spec §15: `delivery.park_after` (duration, default `30s`, above zero), `delivery.max_parked_events` (default `10000000`, `0` unlimited), the required `state_store` section (`type: s3` with `bucket`, `prefix` (default empty), `region`, `max_concurrency`; or `type: filesystem` with `path`, `max_concurrency`; `max_concurrency` defaults to 32 and must be above zero) and the optional `payload_store` section (`type: s3` with `bucket`, `prefix`, `region`). Environment overrides reach every key, including a whole `state_store` supplied only through the environment. Because the section is required (R55), the CLI test configs gain a filesystem store in a temporary directory, and the provider test's inline config gains one too; the binary ignores the stores until Task 7.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/config.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the existing `config` helpers (`de_string`, `de_opt_string`, `de_count`, `de_duration`, `ConfigError::InvalidValue`).
- Produces: `DEFAULT_PARK_AFTER: Duration`, `DEFAULT_MAX_PARKED_EVENTS: u64`, `DEFAULT_STORE_CONCURRENCY: usize`; `DeliveryConfig::{park_after: Duration, max_parked_events: u64}`; `Config::{state_store: StateStoreConfig, payload_store: Option<PayloadStoreConfig>}`; `enum StateStoreConfig { S3(S3StoreConfig), Filesystem(FilesystemStoreConfig) }` with `fn max_concurrency(&self) -> usize`; `struct S3StoreConfig { bucket: String, prefix: String, region: Option<String>, max_concurrency: usize }`; `struct FilesystemStoreConfig { path: PathBuf, max_concurrency: usize }`; `enum PayloadStoreConfig { S3(S3PayloadConfig) }`; `struct S3PayloadConfig { bucket: String, prefix: String, region: Option<String> }`. In `tests/cli.rs`: `struct ConfigFile { file, _state }` with `fn path(&self) -> &Path`, returned by `write_config` and `write_config_with_sink`.

```diff
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
+#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "snake_case", deny_unknown_fields)]
+pub enum StateStoreConfig {
+    S3(S3StoreConfig),
+    Filesystem(FilesystemStoreConfig),
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
+#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
+pub struct S3StoreConfig {
+    #[serde(deserialize_with = "de_string")]
+    pub bucket: String,
+    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = "de_string")]
+    pub prefix: String,
+    #[serde(default, deserialize_with = "de_opt_string")]
+    pub region: Option<String>,
+    #[serde(default = "default_store_concurrency", deserialize_with = "de_count")]
+    pub max_concurrency: usize,
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Deserialize)]
+#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
+pub struct FilesystemStoreConfig {
+    #[serde(deserialize_with = "de_path")]
+    pub path: PathBuf,
+    #[serde(default = "default_store_concurrency", deserialize_with = "de_count")]
+    pub max_concurrency: usize,
+}
+
+impl StateStoreConfig {
+    pub fn max_concurrency(&self) -> usize {
+        match self {
+            Self::S3(s3) => s3.max_concurrency,
+            Self::Filesystem(filesystem) => filesystem.max_concurrency,
+        }
+    }
+
+    fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), ConfigError> {
+        if self.max_concurrency() == 0 {
+            return Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue {
+                field: "state_store.max_concurrency",
+                value: "0".to_owned(),
+                reason: "must be greater than zero",
+            });
+        }
+        match self {
+            Self::S3(s3) => validate_bucket("state_store.bucket", &s3.bucket),
+            Self::Filesystem(filesystem) if filesystem.path.as_os_str().is_empty() => {
+                Err(ConfigError::InvalidValue {
+                    field: "state_store.path",
+                    value: String::new(),
+                    reason: "must not be empty",
+                })
+            }
+            Self::Filesystem(_) => Ok(()),
+        }
+    }
+}
+

 }

+        let yaml = stores(
+            "state_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: pgcdc-state\n  prefix: orders/\n  region: eu-west-1\n  max_concurrency: 8\npayload_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: pgcdc-payloads\n  prefix: orders/\n  region: eu-west-1\n",
+        );
+        let config = parse(&yaml, with_password()).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(
+            config.state_store,
+            StateStoreConfig::S3(S3StoreConfig {
+                bucket: "pgcdc-state".to_owned(),
+                prefix: "orders/".to_owned(),
+                region: Some("eu-west-1".to_owned()),
+                max_concurrency: 8,
+            })
+        );
+        assert_eq!(
+            config.payload_store,
+            Some(PayloadStoreConfig::S3(S3PayloadConfig {
+                bucket: "pgcdc-payloads".to_owned(),
+                prefix: "orders/".to_owned(),
+                region: Some("eu-west-1".to_owned()),
+            }))
+        );
+        let minimal = stores(
+            "state_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: b\npayload_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: p\n",
+        );
+        let config = parse(&minimal, with_password()).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(
+            config.state_store,
+            StateStoreConfig::S3(S3StoreConfig {
+                bucket: "b".to_owned(),
+                prefix: String::new(),
+                region: None,
+                max_concurrency: DEFAULT_STORE_CONCURRENCY,
+            })
+        );
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn the_state_store_is_required_and_can_come_from_the_environment() {
+        assert!(matches!(
+            parse(&stores(""), with_password()),
+            Err(ConfigError::Invalid(_))
+        ));
+        let mut vars = with_password();
+        vars.extend(env(&[
+            ("PGCDC__STATE_STORE__TYPE", "s3"),
+            ("PGCDC__STATE_STORE__BUCKET", "from-env"),
+            ("PGCDC__STATE_STORE__MAX_CONCURRENCY", "4"),
+        ]));
+        let config = parse(&stores(""), vars).unwrap();
+        assert_eq!(
+            config.state_store,
+            StateStoreConfig::S3(S3StoreConfig {
+                bucket: "from-env".to_owned(),
+                prefix: String::new(),
+                region: None,
+                max_concurrency: 4,
+            })
+        );
+    }
+

+        for (sections, field) in [

+                "state_store:\n  type: filesystem\n  path: /x\n  max_concurrency: 0\n",
+                "state_store.max_concurrency",
+            ),
+            (
+                "state_store:\n  type: filesystem\n  path: \"\"\n",
+                "state_store.path",
+            ),
+            (
+                "state_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: \"\"\n",
+                "state_store.bucket",
+            ),
+            (
+                "state_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: a/b\n",
+                "state_store.bucket",
+            ),
+            (
+                "state_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: b\npayload_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: \"\"\n",
+                "payload_store.bucket",
+            ),
+        ] {
+            let error = parse(&stores(sections), with_password()).unwrap_err();
+            assert!(
+                matches!(&error, ConfigError::InvalidValue { field: actual, .. } if *actual == field),
+                "{sections}: {error}"
+            );
+        }
+        for sections in [
+            "state_store:\n  type: postgres\n",
+            "state_store:\n  type: s3\n",
+            "state_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: b\n  path: /x\n",
+            "state_store:\n  type: filesystem\n  path: /x\npayload_store:\n  type: filesystem\n  path: /y\n",
+            "state_store:\n  type: filesystem\n  path: /x\npayload_store:\n  type: s3\n",
+        ] {
+            assert!(
+                matches!(
+                    parse(&stores(sections), with_password()),
+                    Err(ConfigError::Invalid(_))
+                ),
+                "{sections}"
+            );
+        }
+    }
```

### Task 6: SNS sink follow-ups

**Depends on:** none
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** normal

Folds in two milestone 2 follow-ups that touch the sink. First (R67): preflight logged `limit_from_topic` as `attributes.contains_key("MaximumMessageSize")`, which said `true` while the 256 KiB default was in force for a value of `0` or one that does not parse; `Topic` now records whether a parsed, positive value was used, and the log reports that. Second (R68): a String attribute value with a character SNS and SQS reject reached the API unchanged and would fail the whole entry, which with parking means a dead event; such a value is now omitted with a warning, like a null, for core attributes as well as `attribute_columns`, and spec §11.2 gains one sentence.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/sink/sns.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/sink/mod.rs`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md` (§11.2)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Outbound::encode`, `Topic::from_attributes`, `SnsSink::preflight` (milestone 2).
- Produces: no new public items. `Topic` gains `declared_size: bool`; `sink/mod.rs` gains the private `fn sns_text(text: &str) -> bool`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The tests are the test-module hunks of the two diffs in Step 3: `preflight_reads_fifo_mode_and_the_message_size_limit` now also asserts `declared_size` for each attribute map (a parsed `65536` is used as is), `sns_text_is_the_xml_character_set` pins the allowed set at its boundaries, and `attribute_values_with_characters_sns_rejects_are_omitted` covers a control character in the table name, a bell, a NUL, U+FFFE, a tab (kept) and JSON text whose control character serde_json escapes (kept).

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests and see them fail**

Apply only the test-module hunks first.
Run: `mise exec -- cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --lib -E 'test(/sink::/)'`
Expected: FAIL to compile with "no field `declared_size`" and "cannot find function `sns_text`".

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```diff
--- a/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md
+++ b/docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md
@@ -454,6 +454,7 @@
 - **Message attributes.**
   - Every message carries `schema`, `table` and `op`, plus one attribute per column in `sink.attribute_columns`.
   - A column's value comes from `after`, or from `before` when there is no `after`. It is sent as String or Number and omitted when null.
+  - A String value containing a character SNS and SQS reject (anything outside #x9, #xA, #xD, #x20–#xD7FF, #xE000–#xFFFD and #x10000–#x10FFFF) is omitted like a null, with a warning.
   - SNS allows 10 attributes per message.
 
 ### 11.3 StateStore
```

### Task 7: Stores in the session, supervisor and binary

**Depends on:** Task 2, Task 3, Task 4, Task 5
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** high

Threads the stores from configuration to the lanes before the lanes use them (R51). `lane.rs` gains `Stores` and `LaneSettings`, and `Lanes::spawn` takes both (the lanes ignore the stores until Task 8). `SessionConfig` gains `park_after`, `max_parked_events` and `store_concurrency` with `lane_settings()`, and `run_session` takes `&Stores`. The supervisor's `LiveSession` carries the stores, and `store_ready` retries `read_leader` with the reconnect backoff until the state store answers (R56), sharing one retry loop with sink preflight. `pg-cdc run` builds the configured state and payload stores (cancellable, like the sink) and runs the store check after sink preflight, before opening the replication connection. The stream test harness gives every session an in-memory store. This task also pins the milestone 2 follow-up R69: `is_fatal` for every ordering and lane failure, and that the supervisor stops at once on each.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/session.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/supervisor.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/main.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/support.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FilesystemStateStore::new` (Task 2), `S3StateStore::new` and `s3_client` (Task 3), `S3PayloadStore::new` and `PayloadStore` (Task 4), `StateStoreConfig`, `PayloadStoreConfig` and the delivery keys (Task 5), `MemoryStateStore` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `pg_cdc::lane::Stores { pub state: Arc<dyn StateStore>, pub payload: Option<Arc<dyn PayloadStore>> }` (`Clone`, `Debug`); `pg_cdc::lane::LaneSettings { pub lanes: usize, pub park_after: Duration, pub max_parked_events: u64, pub store_concurrency: usize }` (`Copy`); `Lanes::spawn(settings: LaneSettings, sink: &Arc<dyn Sink>, stores: &Stores, permits: &Arc<Semaphore>, reports: &mpsc::UnboundedSender<Report>) -> Lanes`.
  - `SessionConfig::{park_after: Duration, max_parked_events: u64, store_concurrency: usize}`, `SessionConfig::lane_settings(&self) -> LaneSettings`; `run_session(config: &SessionConfig, provider: &dyn ConnectionProvider, sink: &Arc<dyn Sink>, stores: &Stores, shutdown: &CancellationToken)`.
  - `LiveSession::stores: &'a Stores`; `supervisor::store_ready(store: &dyn StateStore, shutdown: &CancellationToken) -> bool`.
  - Stream harness: `support::memory_stores() -> Stores`; `support::config` sets `park_after: DEFAULT_PARK_AFTER`, `max_parked_events: DEFAULT_MAX_PARKED_EVENTS`, `store_concurrency: 4`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

The supervisor tests are the test-module hunks of the `supervisor.rs` diff in Step 3 (a `FlakyStore` that fails `read_leader` a set number of times, the store check's retry and shutdown behavior, and the R69 pins). The CLI test below starts `pg-cdc run` with the discard sink and an S3 state store whose bucket does not exist on moto.

```diff
+
+#[tokio::test]
+async fn a_missing_state_bucket_keeps_the_store_check_retrying_without_taking_the_slot()
+-> TestResult {
+    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
+    prepare(&pg, true).await?;
+    let aws = AwsEmulator::start().await?;
+    let sections = "sink:\n  type: discard\nstate_store:\n  type: s3\n  bucket: missing-state\n";
+    let config = write_config_with_sink(&pg, "cli_slot", "200ms", sections)?;
+    let mut child = spawn_with_env(config.path(), pg.password(), Stdio::null(), &moto_env(&aws))?;
+    let mut stdout = BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().ok_or("no stdout")?).lines();
+    let failed = wait_for_line(&mut stdout, "state store check failed").await?;
+    assert!(failed.contains("NoSuchBucket"), "{failed}");
+    let state = pg.slot_state("cli_slot").await?.ok_or("slot missing")?;
+    assert!(
+        !state.active,
+        "pg-cdc took the slot before its state store answered"
+    );
+    assert!(
+        child.try_wait()?.is_none(),
+        "pg-cdc must keep retrying, not exit"
+    );
+    sigterm(&child).await?;
+    assert!(tokio::time::timeout(WAIT, child.wait()).await??.success());
+    Ok(())
+}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests and see them fail**

Run: `mise exec -- cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test cli -E 'test(missing_state_bucket)'`
Expected: FAIL: the binary still ignores the state store, so it takes the slot and never logs "state store check failed" (the test times out waiting for that line).

```diff
 pub async fn preflight(sink: &dyn Sink, shutdown: &CancellationToken) -> bool {
+    retry_until_ok("sink preflight failed; retrying", shutdown, || {
+        sink.preflight()

+    .await
+}

+    #[derive(Debug, Default)]
+    struct FlakyStore {
+        inner: MemoryStateStore,
+        failures_left: AtomicU32,
+        checks: AtomicU32,
+    }
+
+    #[async_trait]
+    impl StateStore for FlakyStore {
+        async fn append_parked(&self, events: &[ParkedEvent]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
+            self.inner.append_parked(events).await
+        }
+
+        async fn load_parked_heads(&self, partition: u8) -> Result<Vec<ParkedHead>, StoreError> {
+            self.inner.load_parked_heads(partition).await
+        }
+
+        async fn read_parked(
+            &self,
+            key: &OrderKeyId,
+            after: Option<&EventId>,
+            limit: usize,
+        ) -> Result<Vec<ParkedEvent>, StoreError> {
+            self.inner.read_parked(key, after, limit).await
+        }
+
+        async fn set_parked_status(
+            &self,
+            key: &OrderKeyId,
+            id: &EventId,
+            status: ParkStatus,
+        ) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
+            self.inner.set_parked_status(key, id, status).await
+        }
+
+        async fn remove_parked(&self, events: &[(OrderKeyId, EventId)]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
+            self.inner.remove_parked(events).await
+        }
+
+        async fn read_leader(&self) -> Result<Option<Versioned<LeaderRecord>>, StoreError> {
+            self.checks.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
+            let left = self.failures_left.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
+            if left > 0 {
+                self.failures_left.store(left - 1, Ordering::SeqCst);
+                return Err(StoreError::Unavailable("NoSuchBucket".to_owned()));
+            }
+            self.inner.read_leader().await
+        }
+
+        async fn swap_leader(
+            &self,
+            next: &LeaderRecord,
+            expected: Option<&Version>,
+        ) -> Result<SwapOutcome, StoreError> {
+            self.inner.swap_leader(next, expected).await
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
+    async fn the_store_check_retries_with_backoff_until_the_store_answers() {
+        let store = FlakyStore {
+            failures_left: AtomicU32::new(2),
+            ..FlakyStore::default()
+        };
+        assert!(store_ready(&store, &CancellationToken::new()).await);
+        assert_eq!(store.checks.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 3);
+    }
+
+    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
+    async fn shutdown_stops_a_failing_store_check() {
+        let store = FlakyStore {
+            failures_left: AtomicU32::new(u32::MAX),
+            ..FlakyStore::default()
+        };
+        let shutdown = CancellationToken::new();
+        let token = shutdown.clone();
+        tokio::spawn(async move {
+            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(120)).await;
+            token.cancel();
+        });
+        assert!(!store_ready(&store, &shutdown).await);
+        assert!(store.checks.load(Ordering::SeqCst) >= 3);
+    }
+
+    #[test]
+    fn o2_a2_ordering_ack_and_lane_failures_are_fatal() {
+        let out_of_order = SessionError::Lane(LaneError::OutOfOrder {
+            lane: 0,
+            previous: EventId::new(Lsn(2), 0),
+            id: EventId::new(Lsn(1), 0),
+        });
+        for error in [
+            out_of_order,
+            SessionError::LaneStopped(LaneStopped(3)),
+            SessionError::LanesStopped,
+            SessionError::Ack(AckError::NotOpen),
+        ] {
+            assert!(error.is_fatal(), "{error}");
+        }
+        for error in [
+            SessionError::StreamEnded,
+            SessionError::SlotMissing("s".to_owned()),
+        ] {
+            assert!(!error.is_fatal(), "{error}");
+        }
+    }
+
+    #[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]

+                lane: 1,
+                previous: EventId::new(Lsn(9), 0),
+                id: EventId::new(Lsn(8), 0),
+            }),
+            SessionError::LaneStopped(LaneStopped(1)),
+            SessionError::LanesStopped,
+        ] {
+            let mut attempt = Scripted::new(vec![Err(error), Ok(())]);
+            let result = supervise(&mut attempt, &CancellationToken::new()).await;
+            assert!(
+                matches!(result, Err(SupervisorError::Fatal(_))),
+                "{result:?}"
+            );
+            assert_eq!(attempt.runs, 1);
+        }
     }
```

```diff
+async fn stores(config: &Config) -> Stores {
+    let state: Arc<dyn StateStore> = match &config.state_store {

+            s3_client(s3.region.clone()).await,

+        StateStoreConfig::Filesystem(filesystem) => Arc::new(FilesystemStateStore::new(
+            &filesystem.path,
+            &config.pipeline,
+            filesystem.max_concurrency,
+        )),
+    };
+    let payload: Option<Arc<dyn PayloadStore>> = match &config.payload_store {
+        Some(PayloadStoreConfig::S3(s3)) => Some(Arc::new(S3PayloadStore::new(
+            s3_client(s3.region.clone()).await,
+            s3.bucket.clone(),
+            &s3.prefix,
+            &config.pipeline,
+        ))),
+        None => None,
+    };
+    Stores { state, payload }
+}
```

### Task 8: Lanes drain parked queues

**Depends on:** Task 1, Task 7
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Makes lanes honor queues that already exist in the state store (spec §10.3, §10.6, O3): a loader lists the 256 partitions with at most `store_concurrency` in flight and hands each lane its keys' heads before that partition's `Loaded` signal (R54); a lane sends, drains and parks nothing of a key before its partition loads, and releases waiting keys in arrival order; a key with a stored queue appends every new event behind it (settling through `Ticket::park`), settles replays below its floor through `Ticket::skip_replayed` (R53, R61), and drains the queue: it reads up to one batch of heads concurrently (R60), sends them, removes delivered heads in one call, reads the next head, and returns to the live path when the store reports the queue empty. A dead head blocks its key (§10.4); a head the sink rejects for good is marked dead with `set_parked_status`. Store failures back off the lane's store work without resending anything (R57). Nothing parks yet; Task 9 adds that.

`lane.rs` becomes a module directory: `lane/mod.rs` keeps the milestone 2 lane with the key state extended, `lane/loader.rs` loads heads, `lane/stored.rs` holds the stored-key behavior, and the milestone 2 tests move to `lane/tests/mod.rs` with a fault-injecting `TestStore` added to their harness.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/ack.rs`
- Delete: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane.rs` (its code is replaced by `lane/mod.rs`; its test module moves to `lane/tests/mod.rs`)
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/loader.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/stored.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/stored.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/main.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/support.rs`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/parking.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Stores`, `LaneSettings`, `Lanes::spawn` signature (Task 7); `StateStore`, `ParkedEvent::outbound`, `ParkedHead`, `ParkStatus`, `StoreError`, `PARTITIONS`, `now_ms`, `OrderKeyId::partition`, `MemoryStateStore` (Task 1).
- Produces:
  - `Ticket::park(self, appended: &Result<(), StoreError>) -> Result<Settled, Ticket>` and `Ticket::skip_replayed(self, id: EventId, floor: EventId) -> Result<Settled, Ticket>`.
  - `pg_cdc::lane` keeps every public item of milestone 2 and Task 7 with the same signatures (`Pending`, `Report`, `LaneError`, `LaneStopped`, `Stores`, `LaneSettings`, `Lanes::{spawn, dispatch, shutdown}`, `LINGER`, `RETRY_BASE`, `RETRY_CAP`). Internal to the module and used by Tasks 9 and 10: `enum Inbound { Event, Head, Loaded }`, `struct Key { queue, backoff, failing, stored: Option<Stored> }`, `struct Stored { floor: Option<EventId>, head: Option<Outbound>, dead: bool }`, the `Lane` fields `store`, `loaded`, `to_append`, `to_load`, `to_remove`, `to_kill`, `heads_in_memory`, `store_backoff`, `store_due`, and the methods `make_ready`, `front`, `is_loaded`, `store_work`, `store_wake`, `complete_stored`, `accept_head`, `partition_loaded`.
  - Lane test harness (`lane/tests/mod.rs`): `TestStore` (wraps `MemoryStateStore`; `hold(partition)`, `release: Notify`, `load_delay`, `loads`, `peak_loading`, `appends`, `fail_appends`, `fail_reads`, `fail_removes` (counts of calls to fail), `peak_outstanding`, `seed(key, ordinal, status)`, `queue(key) -> Vec<(u32, ParkStatus)>`), `parked(key, ordinal, status) -> ParkedEvent`, `Setup { lanes, concurrency, park_after, max_parked_events, store_concurrency }`, `Harness::{new, start(setup, sink, store), delivered(count)}`, `take_failure`, `outage()`.
  - Stream harness: `support::LIMITS`, `support::spawn_with_stores(params, config, stores) -> Running`, `support::spawn_full(params, config, stores, limits: SinkCapabilities) -> Running`, `support::order_key(id: i64) -> Vec<u8>` (the canonical `["public","orders",<id>]`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Move the milestone 2 lane tests first, so they keep running against the new module. Create `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/` and extract the `mod tests` body of `lane.rs`, dedented by four spaces:

```bash
mkdir -p crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests
python3 - <<'EOF'
source = open("crates/pg-cdc/src/lane.rs").read()
start = source.index("#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n")
body = source[start + len("#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n"):].rstrip()[:-1]
lines = [line[4:] if line.startswith("    ") else line for line in body.split("\n")]
open("crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/mod.rs", "w").write("\n".join(lines).rstrip() + "\n")
EOF
```

Then apply this diff to the extracted file. It declares `mod stored;`, adds `TestStore`, `Setup` and `Harness::start`, makes `Harness::new` async with the 1 ms startup wait (R71), and adds `.await` to every `Harness::new(...)` call.

```diff
+#[derive(Debug, Default)]
+struct TestStore {
+    inner: MemoryStateStore,
+    held: Mutex<Option<u8>>,
+    release: Notify,
+    load_delay: Duration,
+    loads: AtomicUsize,
+    loading: AtomicUsize,
+    peak_loading: AtomicUsize,
+    appends: AtomicUsize,
+    fail_appends: AtomicU32,
+    fail_reads: AtomicU32,
+    fail_removes: AtomicU32,
+    outstanding: Mutex<HashSet<OrderKeyId>>,
+    peak_outstanding: AtomicUsize,
+}
+
+fn take_failure(counter: &AtomicU32) -> bool {
+    counter
+        .try_update(Ordering::SeqCst, Ordering::SeqCst, |left| {
+            left.checked_sub(1)
+        })
+        .is_ok()
+}
+
+fn outage() -> StoreError {
+    StoreError::Unavailable("injected outage".to_owned())
+}
+
+impl TestStore {
+    fn hold(&self, partition: u8) {
+        *self.held.lock().unwrap() = Some(partition);
+    }
+
+    async fn seed(&self, key: &[u8], ordinal: u32, status: ParkStatus) {
+        self.inner
+            .append_parked(&[parked(key, ordinal, status)])
+            .await
+            .unwrap();
+    }
+
+    async fn queue(&self, key: &[u8]) -> Vec<(u32, ParkStatus)> {
+        self.inner
+            .read_parked(&OrderKeyId::of(key), None, usize::MAX)
+            .await
+            .unwrap()
+            .iter()
+            .map(|event| (event.id.ordinal(), event.status))
+            .collect()
+    }
+}
+
+#[async_trait]
+impl StateStore for TestStore {
+    async fn append_parked(&self, events: &[ParkedEvent]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
+        if take_failure(&self.fail_appends) {
+            return Err(outage());
+        }
+        self.appends.fetch_add(events.len(), Ordering::SeqCst);
+        self.inner.append_parked(events).await
+    }
+
+    async fn load_parked_heads(&self, partition: u8) -> Result<Vec<ParkedHead>, StoreError> {
+        let now = self.loading.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst) + 1;
+        self.peak_loading.fetch_max(now, Ordering::SeqCst);
+        tokio::time::sleep(self.load_delay).await;
+        let held = *self.held.lock().unwrap() == Some(partition);
+        if held {
+            self.release.notified().await;
+        }
+        self.loading.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
+        self.loads.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
+        self.inner.load_parked_heads(partition).await
+    }
+
+    async fn read_parked(
+        &self,
+        key: &OrderKeyId,
+        after: Option<&EventId>,
+        limit: usize,
+    ) -> Result<Vec<ParkedEvent>, StoreError> {
+        if take_failure(&self.fail_reads) {
+            return Err(outage());
+        }
+        let events = self.inner.read_parked(key, after, limit).await?;
+        if !events.is_empty() {
+            let mut outstanding = self.outstanding.lock().unwrap();
+            outstanding.insert(*key);
+            self.peak_outstanding
+                .fetch_max(outstanding.len(), Ordering::SeqCst);
+        }
+        Ok(events)
+    }
+
+    async fn set_parked_status(
+        &self,
+        key: &OrderKeyId,
+        id: &EventId,
+        status: ParkStatus,
+    ) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
+        self.outstanding.lock().unwrap().remove(key);
+        self.inner.set_parked_status(key, id, status).await
+    }
+
+    async fn remove_parked(&self, events: &[(OrderKeyId, EventId)]) -> Result<(), StoreError> {
+        if take_failure(&self.fail_removes) {
+            return Err(outage());
+        }
+        {
+            let mut outstanding = self.outstanding.lock().unwrap();
+            for (key, _) in events {
+                outstanding.remove(key);
+            }
+        }
+        self.inner.remove_parked(events).await
+    }
+
+    async fn read_leader(&self) -> Result<Option<Versioned<LeaderRecord>>, StoreError> {
+        self.inner.read_leader().await
+    }
+
+    async fn swap_leader(
+        &self,
+        next: &LeaderRecord,
+        expected: Option<&Version>,
+    ) -> Result<SwapOutcome, StoreError> {
+        self.inner.swap_leader(next, expected).await
+    }
+}
+
+fn parked(key: &[u8], ordinal: u32, status: ParkStatus) -> ParkedEvent {
+    ParkedEvent {
+        order_key: OrderKeyId::of(key),
+        id: EventId::new(Lsn(0x10), ordinal),
+        status,
+        attempts: 1,
+        first_failure_ms: 1,
+        last_error: "seeded".to_owned(),
+        attributes: Vec::new(),
+        body: Bytes::from_static(b"x"),
+    }
+}
+
+#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
+struct Setup {
+    lanes: usize,
+    concurrency: usize,
+    park_after: Duration,
+    max_parked_events: u64,
+    store_concurrency: usize,
+}
+

+            concurrency: 1,

+            max_parked_events: 0,
+            store_concurrency: 4,
+        }
+    }
+}
```
