`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/stored.rs` (new file):

```rust
use super::*;

fn key_outside_partition(partition: u8) -> Vec<u8> {
    (0u8..=255)
        .map(|byte| vec![b'k', byte])
        .find(|key| OrderKeyId::of(key).partition() != partition)
        .unwrap()
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn o3_a_partition_delivers_nothing_until_its_heads_load() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    let held = OrderKeyId::of(b"held").partition();
    store.hold(held);
    let free = key_outside_partition(held);
    let mut harness = Harness::start(Setup::default(), ScriptedSink::delivering(), store).await;
    harness.send(0, b"held", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(1, &free, 10).unwrap();
    harness.settle(1).await;
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(60)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.batches(), vec![vec![1]]);
    harness.store.release.notify_one();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(101));
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.batches(), vec![vec![1], vec![0]]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn parked_queues_drain_in_id_order_then_the_key_returns_to_live() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    for ordinal in 1..=3 {
        store.seed(b"k", ordinal, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    }
    let mut harness = Harness::start(Setup::default(), ScriptedSink::delivering(), store).await;
    harness.send(4, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(5, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(2).await, Lsn(101));
    assert_eq!(harness.delivered(5).await, vec![1, 2, 3, 4, 5]);
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(1)).await;
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"k").await, vec![]);
    let appended = harness.store.appends.load(Ordering::SeqCst);
    harness.send(6, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(102));
    assert_eq!(harness.delivered(6).await, vec![1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]);
    assert_eq!(harness.store.appends.load(Ordering::SeqCst), appended);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn o3_new_events_queue_behind_a_failing_parked_head() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    store.seed(b"k", 1, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    let script: Script = Box::new(|ordinal, attempt| {
        if ordinal == 1 && attempt <= 3 {
            Outcome::Retryable(SinkError("throttled".to_owned()))
        } else {
            Outcome::Delivered
        }
    });
    let mut harness =
        Harness::start(Setup::default(), ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, script), store).await;
    harness.send(2, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(3, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(2).await, Lsn(101));
    assert_eq!(harness.delivered(3).await, vec![1, 2, 3]);
    let order: Vec<u32> = harness
        .sink
        .sends()
        .iter()
        .map(|sent| sent.ordinal)
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(order, vec![1, 1, 1, 1, 2, 3]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn replayed_events_below_the_head_settle_without_being_sent() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    store.seed(b"k", 5, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    store.seed(b"k", 7, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    store.hold(OrderKeyId::of(b"k").partition());
    let mut harness = Harness::start(Setup::default(), ScriptedSink::delivering(), store).await;
    for ordinal in [3, 5, 7, 9] {
        harness.send(ordinal, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    }
    harness.store.release.notify_one();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(4).await, Lsn(103));
    assert_eq!(harness.delivered(3).await, vec![5, 7, 9]);
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(60)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.sends().len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"k").await, vec![]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_dead_head_blocks_only_its_key() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    store.seed(b"k", 1, ParkStatus::Dead).await;
    store.seed(b"k", 2, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    let mut harness = Harness::start(Setup::default(), ScriptedSink::delivering(), store).await;
    harness.send(3, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(4, b"other", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(2).await, Lsn(101));
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(600)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.batches(), vec![vec![4]]);
    assert_eq!(
        harness.store.queue(b"k").await,
        vec![
            (1, ParkStatus::Dead),
            (2, ParkStatus::Retrying),
            (3, ParkStatus::Retrying)
        ]
    );
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_terminal_outcome_on_a_parked_head_marks_it_dead() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    store.seed(b"k", 1, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    store.seed(b"k", 2, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    let script: Script = Box::new(|ordinal, _| {
        if ordinal == 1 {
            Outcome::Terminal(SinkError("rejected".to_owned()))
        } else {
            Outcome::Delivered
        }
    });
    let mut harness =
        Harness::start(Setup::default(), ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, script), store).await;
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(600)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.batches(), vec![vec![1]]);
    assert_eq!(
        harness.store.queue(b"k").await,
        vec![(1, ParkStatus::Dead), (2, ParkStatus::Retrying)]
    );
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_store_outage_while_draining_resends_nothing_and_resumes() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    store.seed(b"k", 1, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    store.seed(b"k", 2, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    store.fail_removes.store(3, Ordering::SeqCst);
    store.fail_reads.store(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let harness = Harness::start(Setup::default(), ScriptedSink::delivering(), store).await;
    assert_eq!(harness.delivered(2).await, vec![1, 2]);
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(60)).await;
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.sends().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"k").await, vec![]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn startup_loading_is_bounded_by_the_store_concurrency() {
    let store = TestStore {
        load_delay: Duration::from_millis(10),
        ..TestStore::default()
    };
    let setup = Setup {
        store_concurrency: 3,
        ..Setup::default()
    };
    let harness = Harness::start(setup, ScriptedSink::delivering(), store).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5)).await;
    assert_eq!(harness.store.loads.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 256);
    assert_eq!(harness.store.peak_loading.load(Ordering::SeqCst), 3);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn parked_heads_in_memory_never_exceed_one_batch() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    for key in 0u8..40 {
        store
            .seed(&[key], u32::from(key), ParkStatus::Retrying)
            .await;
    }
    let limits = SinkCapabilities {
        max_batch_messages: 4,
        ..LIMITS
    };
    let mut sink = ScriptedSink::new(limits, Box::new(|_, _| Outcome::Delivered));
    sink.latency = Duration::from_millis(10);
    let harness = Harness::start(Setup::default(), sink, store).await;
    let mut delivered = harness.delivered(40).await;
    delivered.sort_unstable();
    assert_eq!(delivered, (0..40).collect::<Vec<u32>>());
    assert!(harness.store.peak_outstanding.load(Ordering::SeqCst) <= 4);
    assert!(harness.sink.batches().iter().all(|batch| batch.len() <= 4));
}
```

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use bytes::Bytes;
use pg_cdc::event::EventId;
use pg_cdc::lane::Stores;
use pg_cdc::order::{OrderBy, OrderKeyId};
use pg_cdc::session::SessionConfig;
use pg_cdc::state::memory::MemoryStateStore;
use pg_cdc::state::{ParkStatus, ParkedEvent, StateStore};
use pg_cdc_decoder::Lsn;
use pg_cdc_testkit::Postgres;

use crate::support::{self, TestResult};

const FAST: Duration = Duration::from_millis(200);
const BUFFER: usize = 1 << 16;

async fn orders(pg: &Postgres) -> TestResult {
    support::prepare(
        pg,
        &["CREATE TABLE orders (id int PRIMARY KEY, status text)"],
        &["orders"],
    )
    .await
}

fn keyed(park_after: Duration) -> SessionConfig {
    let mut config = support::config(FAST, BUFFER);
    config.order_by = OrderBy::Key;
    config.lanes = 4;
    config.park_after = park_after;
    config
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn o3_a_parked_queue_drains_before_new_events_for_its_key() -> TestResult {
    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
    orders(&pg).await?;
    let store = MemoryStateStore::new();
    store
        .append_parked(&[ParkedEvent {
            order_key: OrderKeyId::of(&support::order_key(1)),
            id: EventId::new(Lsn(1), 0),
            status: ParkStatus::Retrying,
            attempts: 3,
            first_failure_ms: 1,
            last_error: "throttled".to_owned(),
            attributes: Vec::new(),
            body: Bytes::from_static(br#"{"seeded":true}"#),
        }])
        .await?;
    let stores = Stores {
        state: Arc::new(store.clone()),
        payload: None,
    };
    let mut session =
        support::spawn_with_stores(support::params(&pg), keyed(Duration::from_secs(30)), stores);
    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'new')").await?;
    let first = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(first, serde_json::json!({"seeded": true}));
    let second = session.next_event().await?;
    assert_eq!(second["after"]["status"], "new");
    assert_eq!(store.parked_events(), 0);
    session.stop().await
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests and see them fail**

Run: `mise exec -- cargo nextest run -p pg-cdc --test stream -E 'test(o3_a_parked_queue_drains_before_new_events_for_its_key)'`
Expected: FAIL: the first event the sink receives is the new insert, not the seeded parked event (`assertion left == right failed`), because the milestone 2 lanes ignore the store. The moved and new lane tests under `lane/tests/` are not compiled yet: `lane.rs` still declares its own inline test module, so nothing includes `lane/tests/mod.rs` until Step 3 replaces `lane.rs` with `lane/mod.rs`; the ack tests arrive with the `ack.rs` diff in Step 3.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Delete `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane.rs` (`git rm crates/pg-cdc/src/lane.rs`) and create the module. `lane/mod.rs` keeps milestone 2's batching, linger, retries and the O2 check; what is new is the `Inbound` enum, the partition gate in `make_ready`, `front` (a stored key's loaded head or a live key's queue front), the store-work step in the run loop, and `complete_stored` for drained heads.

`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/mod.rs` (new file):

```rust
mod loader;
mod stored;

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests;

use std::cmp::Reverse;
use std::collections::hash_map::Entry;
use std::collections::{BTreeSet, BinaryHeap, HashMap, VecDeque};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use tokio::sync::{Semaphore, mpsc};
use tokio::task::JoinSet;
use tokio::time::Instant;

use crate::ack::{Settled, Ticket};
use crate::event::EventId;
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;
use crate::payload::PayloadStore;
use crate::sink::{Outbound, Outcome, Sink, SinkCapabilities, SinkError};
use crate::state::{PARTITIONS, ParkedHead, StateStore};
use crate::supervisor::Backoff;

pub const LINGER: Duration = Duration::from_millis(5);
pub const RETRY_BASE: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);
pub const RETRY_CAP: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Pending {
    pub outbound: Outbound,
    pub ticket: Ticket,
    pub arrived: Instant,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum Report {
    Settled(Settled),
    Fatal(LaneError),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
pub enum LaneError {
    #[error("event {id} reached lane {lane} after event {previous}")]
    OutOfOrder {
        lane: usize,
        previous: EventId,
        id: EventId,
    },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, thiserror::Error)]
#[error("lane {0} has stopped")]
pub struct LaneStopped(pub usize);

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Stores {
    pub state: Arc<dyn StateStore>,
    pub payload: Option<Arc<dyn PayloadStore>>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct LaneSettings {
    pub lanes: usize,
    pub park_after: Duration,
    pub max_parked_events: u64,
    pub store_concurrency: usize,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
enum Inbound {
    Event(Pending),
    Head(ParkedHead),
    Loaded(u8),
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Lanes {
    inboxes: Vec<mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>>,
    tasks: JoinSet<()>,
}

impl Lanes {
    pub fn spawn(
        settings: LaneSettings,
        sink: &Arc<dyn Sink>,
        stores: &Stores,
        permits: &Arc<Semaphore>,
        reports: &mpsc::UnboundedSender<Report>,
    ) -> Self {
        let capabilities = sink.capabilities();
        let mut tasks = JoinSet::new();
        let inboxes: Vec<_> = (0..settings.lanes.max(1))
            .map(|index| {
                let (inbox, receiver) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
                let lane = Lane {
                    index,
                    sink: Arc::clone(sink),
                    store: Arc::clone(&stores.state),
                    permits: Arc::clone(permits),
                    capabilities,
                    inbox: receiver,
                    reports: reports.clone(),
                    keys: HashMap::new(),
                    ready: VecDeque::new(),
                    retries: BinaryHeap::new(),
                    last: None,
                    loaded: [false; PARTITIONS],
                    to_append: BTreeSet::new(),
                    to_load: VecDeque::new(),
                    to_remove: Vec::new(),
                    to_kill: Vec::new(),
                    heads_in_memory: 0,
                    store_backoff: Backoff::new(RETRY_BASE, RETRY_CAP),
                    store_due: None,
                };
                tasks.spawn(lane.run());
                inbox
            })
            .collect();
        tasks.spawn(loader::load(
            Arc::clone(&stores.state),
            inboxes.clone(),
            settings.store_concurrency,
        ));
        Self { inboxes, tasks }
    }

    pub fn dispatch(&self, pending: Pending) -> Result<(), LaneStopped> {
        let index = pending.outbound.order_key.lane(self.inboxes.len());
        let inbox = self.inboxes.get(index).ok_or(LaneStopped(index))?;
        inbox
            .send(Inbound::Event(pending))
            .map_err(|_| LaneStopped(index))
    }

    pub async fn shutdown(mut self) {
        self.tasks.shutdown().await;
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct Key {
    queue: VecDeque<Pending>,
    backoff: Backoff,
    failing: bool,
    stored: Option<Stored>,
}

impl Key {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            queue: VecDeque::new(),
            backoff: Backoff::new(RETRY_BASE, RETRY_CAP),
            failing: false,
            stored: None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct Stored {
    floor: Option<EventId>,
    head: Option<Outbound>,
    dead: bool,
}

struct Lane {
    index: usize,
    sink: Arc<dyn Sink>,
    store: Arc<dyn StateStore>,
    permits: Arc<Semaphore>,
    capabilities: SinkCapabilities,
    inbox: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Inbound>,
    reports: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Report>,
    keys: HashMap<OrderKeyId, Key>,
    ready: VecDeque<OrderKeyId>,
    retries: BinaryHeap<Reverse<(Instant, OrderKeyId)>>,
    last: Option<EventId>,
    loaded: [bool; PARTITIONS],
    to_append: BTreeSet<OrderKeyId>,
    to_load: VecDeque<OrderKeyId>,
    to_remove: Vec<(OrderKeyId, EventId)>,
    to_kill: Vec<(OrderKeyId, EventId)>,
    heads_in_memory: usize,
    store_backoff: Backoff,
    store_due: Option<Instant>,
}

impl Lane {
    async fn run(mut self) {
        loop {
            while let Ok(inbound) = self.inbox.try_recv() {
                if !self.accept(inbound) {
                    return;
                }
            }
            let now = Instant::now();
            self.promote_due_retries(now);
            if self.store_work(now).await {
                continue;
            }
            let (count, full) = self.plan_batch();
            let linger = self.linger_deadline();
            if let Some(deadline) = linger
                && (full || Instant::now() >= deadline)
            {
                self.send_batch(count).await;
                continue;
            }
            let next_retry = self.retries.peek().map(|Reverse((due, _))| *due);
            let until = [linger, next_retry, self.store_wake()]
                .into_iter()
                .flatten()
                .min();
            if !self.wait(until).await {
                return;
            }
        }
    }

    fn accept(&mut self, inbound: Inbound) -> bool {
        match inbound {
            Inbound::Event(pending) => self.accept_event(pending),
            Inbound::Head(head) => {
                self.accept_head(head);
                true
            }
            Inbound::Loaded(partition) => {
                self.partition_loaded(partition);
                true
            }
        }
    }

    fn accept_event(&mut self, pending: Pending) -> bool {
        let id = pending.outbound.id;
        if let Some(previous) = self.last
            && id <= previous
        {
            let error = LaneError::OutOfOrder {
                lane: self.index,
                previous,
                id,
            };
            let _ = self.reports.send(Report::Fatal(error));
            return false;
        }
        self.last = Some(id);
        let key = pending.outbound.order_key;
        match self.keys.entry(key) {
            Entry::Occupied(mut entry) => {
                let state = entry.get_mut();
                if let Some(stored) = &state.stored {
                    if let Some(floor) = stored.floor
                        && id < floor
                    {
                        if let Ok(settled) = pending.ticket.skip_replayed(id, floor) {
                            let _ = self.reports.send(Report::Settled(settled));
                        }
                        return true;
                    }
                    self.to_append.insert(key);
                }
                state.queue.push_back(pending);
            }
            Entry::Vacant(entry) => {
                let mut state = Key::new();
                state.queue.push_back(pending);
                entry.insert(state);
                self.make_ready(key);
            }
        }
        true
    }

    fn is_loaded(&self, key: OrderKeyId) -> bool {
        self.loaded
            .get(usize::from(key.partition()))
            .copied()
            .unwrap_or(false)
    }

    fn make_ready(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId) {
        if !self.is_loaded(key) {
            return;
        }
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        let head = match &state.stored {
            Some(stored) => stored.head.as_ref(),
            None => state.queue.front().map(|pending| &pending.outbound),
        };
        let Some(head) = head else {
            return;
        };
        let bytes = head.encoded_len();
        if bytes > self.capabilities.max_message_bytes {
            tracing::error!(
                order_key = %key,
                id = %head.id,
                bytes,
                max_message_bytes = self.capabilities.max_message_bytes,
                "event exceeds the sink's message size; its order key is blocked"
            );
            return;
        }
        self.ready.push_back(key);
    }

    fn promote_due_retries(&mut self, now: Instant) {
        while let Some(Reverse((due, key))) = self.retries.peek().copied() {
            if due > now {
                break;
            }
            self.retries.pop();
            match self.keys.get(&key) {
                Some(Key {
                    stored: Some(_), ..
                }) => self.to_load.push_back(key),
                Some(_) => self.make_ready(key),
                None => {}
            }
        }
    }

    fn front(&self, key: &OrderKeyId) -> Option<&Outbound> {
        let state = self.keys.get(key)?;
        match &state.stored {
            Some(stored) => stored.head.as_ref(),
            None => state.queue.front().map(|pending| &pending.outbound),
        }
    }

    fn linger_deadline(&self) -> Option<Instant> {
        let key = self.ready.front()?;
        let state = self.keys.get(key)?;
        match &state.stored {
            Some(_) => Some(Instant::now()),
            None => Some(state.queue.front()?.arrived + LINGER),
        }
    }

    fn plan_batch(&self) -> (usize, bool) {
        let limits = self.capabilities;
        let mut count = 0;
        let mut bytes = 0_usize;
        for key in &self.ready {
            let Some(head) = self.front(key) else {
                continue;
            };
            let size = head.encoded_len();
            if count == limits.max_batch_messages
                || (count > 0 && bytes.saturating_add(size) > limits.max_batch_bytes)
            {
                return (count, true);
            }
            count += 1;
            bytes = bytes.saturating_add(size);
        }
        (count, count >= limits.max_batch_messages)
    }

    async fn wait(&mut self, until: Option<Instant>) -> bool {
        let timer = async {
            match until {
                Some(at) => tokio::time::sleep_until(at).await,
                None => std::future::pending().await,
            }
        };
        tokio::select! {
            received = self.inbox.recv() => match received {
                Some(inbound) => self.accept(inbound),
                None => false,
            },
            () = timer => true,
        }
    }

    async fn send_batch(&mut self, count: usize) {
        let mut keys = Vec::with_capacity(count);
        let mut batch = Vec::with_capacity(count);
        for key in self.ready.drain(..count).collect::<Vec<_>>() {
            if let Some(head) = self.front(&key) {
                batch.push(head.clone());
                keys.push(key);
            }
        }
        let outcomes = match self.permits.acquire().await {
            Ok(_permit) => self.sink.send(&batch).await,
            Err(_) => Vec::new(),
        };
        if outcomes.len() != batch.len() {
            tracing::error!(
                sent = batch.len(),
                outcomes = outcomes.len(),
                "the sink broke its contract of one outcome per message"
            );
        }
        let mut outcomes = outcomes.into_iter();
        for key in keys {
            let outcome = outcomes.next().unwrap_or_else(|| {
                Outcome::Retryable(SinkError("the sink returned no outcome".to_owned()))
            });
            self.complete(key, &outcome);
        }
    }

    fn complete(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, outcome: &Outcome) {
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        if state.stored.is_some() {
            self.complete_stored(key, outcome);
            return;
        }
        match outcome {
            Outcome::Delivered => {
                let Some(head) = state.queue.pop_front() else {
                    return;
                };
                if let Ok(settled) = head.ticket.settle(outcome) {
                    let _ = self.reports.send(Report::Settled(settled));
                }
                if state.queue.is_empty() {
                    self.keys.remove(&key);
                } else {
                    state.backoff.reset();
                    state.failing = false;
                    self.make_ready(key);
                }
            }
            Outcome::Retryable(error) => {
                let delay = state.backoff.next_delay();
                let id = state.queue.front().map(|head| head.outbound.id);
                if state.failing {
                    tracing::debug!(order_key = %key, ?id, %error, ?delay, "delivery failed again; retrying");
                } else {
                    tracing::warn!(order_key = %key, ?id, %error, ?delay, "delivery failed; retrying");
                }
                state.failing = true;
                self.retries.push(Reverse((Instant::now() + delay, key)));
            }
            Outcome::Terminal(error) => {
                let id = state.queue.front().map(|head| head.outbound.id);
                tracing::error!(
                    order_key = %key,
                    ?id,
                    %error,
                    "the sink rejected the event for good; its order key is blocked"
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/loader.rs` (new file):

```rust
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;

use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use tokio::task::{Id, JoinSet};

use super::{Inbound, RETRY_BASE, RETRY_CAP};
use crate::state::{ParkedHead, StateStore};
use crate::supervisor::Backoff;

pub(super) async fn load(
    store: Arc<dyn StateStore>,
    inboxes: Vec<mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>>,
    concurrency: usize,
) {
    let mut partitions = 0..=u8::MAX;
    let mut running = JoinSet::new();
    let mut started: HashMap<Id, u8> = HashMap::new();
    loop {
        while running.len() < concurrency.max(1) {
            let Some(partition) = partitions.next() else {
                break;
            };
            let handle = running.spawn(load_partition(Arc::clone(&store), partition));
            started.insert(handle.id(), partition);
        }
        let Some(joined) = running.join_next_with_id().await else {
            return;
        };
        match joined {
            Ok((id, (partition, heads))) => {
                started.remove(&id);
                deliver(&inboxes, partition, heads);
            }
            Err(error) => {
                if let Some(partition) = started.remove(&error.id()) {
                    let handle = running.spawn(load_partition(Arc::clone(&store), partition));
                    started.insert(handle.id(), partition);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

async fn load_partition(store: Arc<dyn StateStore>, partition: u8) -> (u8, Vec<ParkedHead>) {
    let mut backoff = Backoff::new(RETRY_BASE, RETRY_CAP);
    loop {
        match store.load_parked_heads(partition).await {
            Ok(heads) => return (partition, heads),
            Err(error) => {
                let delay = backoff.next_delay();
                tracing::warn!(partition, %error, ?delay, "cannot load parked queue heads; retrying");
                tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
            }
        }
    }
}

fn deliver(inboxes: &[mpsc::UnboundedSender<Inbound>], partition: u8, heads: Vec<ParkedHead>) {
    for head in heads {
        if let Some(inbox) = inboxes.get(head.order_key.lane(inboxes.len())) {
            let _ = inbox.send(Inbound::Head(head));
        }
    }
    for inbox in inboxes {
        let _ = inbox.send(Inbound::Loaded(partition));
    }
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/stored.rs` (new file):

```rust
use std::cmp::Reverse;
use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::sync::Arc;

use tokio::task::JoinSet;
use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::{Key, Lane, Report, Stored};
use crate::event::EventId;
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;
use crate::sink::Outcome;
use crate::state::{ParkStatus, ParkedEvent, ParkedHead, StoreError, now_ms};

pub(super) const QUEUED_BEHIND: &str = "queued behind a parked event";

impl Lane {
    pub(super) fn accept_head(&mut self, head: ParkedHead) {
        let key = head.order_key;
        let state = self.keys.entry(key).or_insert_with(Key::new);
        state.stored = Some(Stored {
            floor: Some(head.head),
            head: None,
            dead: false,
        });
        let queued = std::mem::take(&mut state.queue);
        for pending in queued {
            let id = pending.outbound.id;
            if id < head.head {
                if let Ok(settled) = pending.ticket.skip_replayed(id, head.head) {
                    let _ = self.reports.send(Report::Settled(settled));
                }
            } else {
                state.queue.push_back(pending);
            }
        }
        if !state.queue.is_empty() {
            self.to_append.insert(key);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn partition_loaded(&mut self, partition: u8) {
        if let Some(loaded) = self.loaded.get_mut(usize::from(partition)) {
            *loaded = true;
        }
        let mut keys: Vec<(Option<EventId>, OrderKeyId)> = self
            .keys
            .iter()
            .filter(|(key, _)| key.partition() == partition)
            .map(|(key, state)| (state.queue.front().map(|pending| pending.outbound.id), *key))
            .collect();
        keys.sort_unstable();
        for (_, key) in keys {
            match self.keys.get(&key) {
                Some(Key {
                    stored: Some(_), ..
                }) => self.to_load.push_back(key),
                Some(_) => self.make_ready(key),
                None => {}
            }
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn store_wake(&self) -> Option<Instant> {
        let pending = !self.to_append.is_empty()
            || !self.to_remove.is_empty()
            || !self.to_kill.is_empty()
            || (!self.to_load.is_empty() && self.heads_in_memory < self.head_capacity());
        if pending { self.store_due } else { None }
    }

    fn head_capacity(&self) -> usize {
        self.capabilities.max_batch_messages.max(1)
    }

    pub(super) async fn store_work(&mut self, now: Instant) -> bool {
        if self.store_due.is_some_and(|due| due > now) {
            return false;
        }
        if !self.to_append.is_empty() {
            self.flush_appends().await;
        } else if !self.to_remove.is_empty() {
            self.flush_removes().await;
        } else if !self.to_kill.is_empty() {
            self.flush_kills().await;
        } else if !self.to_load.is_empty() && self.heads_in_memory < self.head_capacity() {
            self.load_heads().await;
        } else {
            return false;
        }
        true
    }

    fn store_succeeded(&mut self) {
        self.store_backoff.reset();
        self.store_due = None;
    }

    fn store_failed(&mut self, operation: &str, error: &StoreError) {
        let delay = self.store_backoff.next_delay();
        tracing::warn!(lane = self.index, operation, %error, ?delay, "state store call failed; retrying");
        self.store_due = Some(Instant::now() + delay);
    }

    fn queued_events(&self, keys: &[OrderKeyId]) -> Vec<ParkedEvent> {
        let parked_at = now_ms();
        keys.iter()
            .filter_map(|key| self.keys.get(key))
            .flat_map(|state| state.queue.iter())
            .map(|pending| ParkedEvent {
                order_key: pending.outbound.order_key,
                id: pending.outbound.id,
                status: ParkStatus::Retrying,
                attempts: 0,
                first_failure_ms: parked_at,
                last_error: QUEUED_BEHIND.to_owned(),
                attributes: pending.outbound.attributes.clone(),
                body: pending.outbound.body.clone(),
            })
            .collect()
    }

    async fn flush_appends(&mut self) {
        let keys: Vec<OrderKeyId> = std::mem::take(&mut self.to_append).into_iter().collect();
        let events = self.queued_events(&keys);
        let appended = self.store.append_parked(&events).await;
        if let Err(error) = &appended {
            self.store_failed("append", error);
            self.to_append.extend(keys);
            return;
        }
        self.store_succeeded();
        for key in keys {
            let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
                continue;
            };
            for pending in std::mem::take(&mut state.queue) {
                if let Ok(settled) = pending.ticket.park(&appended) {
                    let _ = self.reports.send(Report::Settled(settled));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn load_heads(&mut self) {
        let room = self.head_capacity().saturating_sub(self.heads_in_memory);
        let mut wanted = Vec::new();
        while wanted.len() < room {
            let Some(key) = self.to_load.pop_front() else {
                break;
            };
            let waiting = self.keys.get(&key).and_then(|state| state.stored.as_ref());
            if waiting.is_some_and(|stored| stored.head.is_none() && !stored.dead)
                && !wanted.contains(&key)
            {
                wanted.push(key);
            }
        }
        let mut reads = JoinSet::new();
        for key in &wanted {
            let (store, key) = (Arc::clone(&self.store), *key);
            reads.spawn(async move { (key, store.read_parked(&key, None, 1).await) });
        }
        let mut answered = HashSet::new();
        let mut failure = None;
        while let Some(joined) = reads.join_next().await {
            let Ok((key, read)) = joined else {
                continue;
            };
            answered.insert(key);
            match read {
                Ok(events) => self.head_read(key, events.into_iter().next()),
                Err(error) => {
                    self.to_load.push_back(key);
                    failure = Some(error);
                }
            }
        }
        for key in wanted {
            if !answered.contains(&key) {
                self.to_load.push_back(key);
            }
        }
        match failure {
            Some(error) => self.store_failed("read", &error),
            None => self.store_succeeded(),
        }
    }

    fn head_read(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, head: Option<ParkedEvent>) {
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(stored) = state.stored.as_mut() else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(head) = head else {
            state.stored = None;
            state.backoff.reset();
            state.failing = false;
            self.to_append.remove(&key);
            if state.queue.is_empty() {
                self.keys.remove(&key);
            } else {
                self.make_ready(key);
            }
            return;
        };
        stored.floor = Some(head.id);
        if head.status == ParkStatus::Dead {
            stored.dead = true;
            tracing::error!(
                order_key = %key,
                id = %head.id,
                error = %head.last_error,
                "a dead event blocks its order key"
            );
            return;
        }
        stored.head = Some(head.outbound());
        self.heads_in_memory += 1;
        self.make_ready(key);
    }

    pub(super) fn complete_stored(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, outcome: &Outcome) {
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        let Some(head) = state.stored.as_mut().and_then(|stored| stored.head.take()) else {
            return;
        };
        self.heads_in_memory = self.heads_in_memory.saturating_sub(1);
        match outcome {
            Outcome::Delivered => {
                state.backoff.reset();
                state.failing = false;
                self.to_remove.push((key, head.id));
            }
            Outcome::Retryable(error) => {
                let delay = state.backoff.next_delay();
                if !state.failing {
                    tracing::warn!(order_key = %key, id = %head.id, %error, ?delay, "redelivery of a parked event failed; retrying");
                }
                state.failing = true;
                self.retries.push(Reverse((Instant::now() + delay, key)));
            }
            Outcome::Terminal(error) => {
                tracing::error!(order_key = %key, id = %head.id, %error, "the sink rejected a parked event for good; marking it dead");
                self.to_kill.push((key, head.id));
            }
        }
    }

    async fn flush_removes(&mut self) {
        let removals = std::mem::take(&mut self.to_remove);
        if let Err(error) = self.store.remove_parked(&removals).await {
            self.store_failed("remove", &error);
            self.to_remove = removals;
            return;
        }
        self.store_succeeded();
        for (key, id) in removals {
            if let Some(stored) = self
                .keys
                .get_mut(&key)
                .and_then(|state| state.stored.as_mut())
            {
                stored.floor = Some(id);
                self.to_load.push_back(key);
            }
        }
    }

    async fn flush_kills(&mut self) {
        while let Some((key, id)) = self.to_kill.pop() {
            if let Err(error) = self
                .store
                .set_parked_status(&key, &id, ParkStatus::Dead)
                .await
            {
                self.store_failed("mark dead", &error);
                self.to_kill.push((key, id));
                return;
            }
            if let Some(stored) = self
                .keys
                .get_mut(&key)
                .and_then(|state| state.stored.as_mut())
            {
                stored.dead = true;
            }
        }
        self.store_succeeded();
    }
}
```

### Task 9: Lanes park failing and rejected events

**Depends on:** Task 8
**Complexity:** complex
**Risk:** high

Adds parking itself (spec §10.2, §10.4, §10.5, R58, R59, R64). A live key records its first failure; its retries are capped at first failure + `park_after`, and when that deadline passes the key starts parking: its whole in-memory queue is appended to the store and settled from the confirmation, and its head is then drained like any stored queue. A `Terminal` outcome parks the head at once as `dead`, with the events behind it as `retrying`; an event above the sink's `max_message_bytes` parks dead too (Task 10 adds pointers). Stored events carry the head's attempts, first failure time and last error. Appends that would exceed `max_parked_events` are deferred on their own timer, so draining continues and frees room; the count starts from the loaded heads. `Lanes::park_all` asks every lane to park all queued events of loaded partitions and to stop sending and loading, for Task 11. A model-based property test (§14.6) drives random keys through flaky, rejected and outage outcomes with failing appends, then restarts on the same store and replays every event, checking per-key order, single delivery, store contents and settlement.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/stored.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/loader.rs`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/park.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/mod.rs`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/park.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/model.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/support.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/parking.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Task 8 lane internals listed there; `LaneSettings::{park_after, max_parked_events}` (Task 7).
- Produces:
  - `Lanes::park_all(&self)`.
  - Internal, used by Task 10: `Inbound::ParkAll`; `Key::failure: Option<Failure>` (replacing `failing`) with `struct Failure { since: Instant, since_ms: u64, attempts: u32, error: String }` and `Key::record_failure(&mut self, error: &SinkError)`; `Stored::dead_on_append`; `Lane::{park_after, max_parked, parked: Arc<AtomicU64>, closing, append_due}`; `Lane::{park_due, retry_live, start_parking(key, dead: bool), oversize(key, bytes), park_all}`.
  - Stream harness: `Mode::RejectContaining(String)` (answers `Terminal` with "test sink rejects the event"), `support::filesystem_stores(path) -> Stores`, `support::parked(stores, key) -> TestResult<Vec<ParkedEvent>>`.

`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/park.rs` (new file):

```rust
use super::*;

fn outage(down: &Arc<AtomicBool>, failing: u32) -> Script {
    let down = Arc::clone(down);
    Box::new(move |ordinal, _| {
        if ordinal == failing && down.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            Outcome::Retryable(SinkError("throttled".to_owned()))
        } else {
            Outcome::Delivered
        }
    })
}

fn parking_after(park_after: Duration) -> Setup {
    Setup {
        park_after,
        ..Setup::default()
    }
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_key_failing_for_park_after_parks_its_events_and_settles_them() {
    let down = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
    let setup = parking_after(Duration::from_secs(1));
    let sink = ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, outage(&down, 0));
    let mut harness = Harness::start(setup, sink, TestStore::default()).await;
    let start = Instant::now();
    harness.send(0, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(1, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(2, b"other", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(3).await, Lsn(102));
    let parked_after = Instant::now() - start;
    assert!(
        parked_after >= Duration::from_secs(1) && parked_after <= Duration::from_millis(1100),
        "parked after {parked_after:?}"
    );
    let queue = harness
        .store
        .inner
        .read_parked(&OrderKeyId::of(b"k"), None, 10)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let summary: Vec<(u32, ParkStatus, u32, &str)> = queue
        .iter()
        .map(|event| {
            (
                event.id.ordinal(),
                event.status,
                event.attempts,
                event.last_error.as_str(),
            )
        })
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(summary.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!((summary[0].0, summary[0].1), (0, ParkStatus::Retrying));
    assert!(summary[0].2 >= 1);
    assert_eq!(summary[0].3, "throttled");
    assert_eq!(
        summary[1],
        (1, ParkStatus::Retrying, 0, "queued behind a parked event")
    );
    let sent: Vec<u32> = harness
        .sink
        .sends()
        .iter()
        .map(|sent| sent.ordinal)
        .collect();
    assert!(!sent.contains(&1), "{sent:?}");
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_parked_key_drains_in_order_once_the_sink_recovers() {
    let down = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
    let setup = parking_after(Duration::from_secs(1));
    let sink = ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, outage(&down, 0));
    let mut harness = Harness::start(setup, sink, TestStore::default()).await;
    harness.send(0, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(1, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(2).await, Lsn(101));
    harness.send(2, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(102));
    down.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    assert_eq!(harness.delivered(3).await, vec![0, 1, 2]);
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(1)).await;
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"k").await, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(harness.store.inner.parked_events(), 0);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_terminal_outcome_parks_the_event_dead_and_blocks_its_key() {
    let script: Script = Box::new(|ordinal, _| {
        if ordinal == 0 {
            Outcome::Terminal(SinkError("rejected".to_owned()))
        } else {
            Outcome::Delivered
        }
    });
    let mut harness = Harness::new(1, 1, ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, script)).await;
    harness.send(0, b"poison", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(1, b"poison", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(2, b"healthy", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(3).await, Lsn(102));
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(600)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.batches(), vec![vec![0, 2]]);
    assert_eq!(
        harness.store.queue(b"poison").await,
        vec![(0, ParkStatus::Dead), (1, ParkStatus::Retrying)]
    );
    harness.send(3, b"poison", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(103));
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"poison").await.len(), 3);
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.batches(), vec![vec![0, 2]]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn an_event_above_the_sink_limit_is_parked_dead_without_a_payload_store() {
    let limits = SinkCapabilities {
        max_message_bytes: 100,
        ..LIMITS
    };
    let mut harness = Harness::new(
        1,
        1,
        ScriptedSink::new(limits, Box::new(|_, _| Outcome::Delivered)),
    )
    .await;
    harness.send(0, b"wide", 101).unwrap();
    harness.send(1, b"narrow", 100).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(2).await, Lsn(101));
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(60)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.batches(), vec![vec![1]]);
    let queue = harness
        .store
        .inner
        .read_parked(&OrderKeyId::of(b"wide"), None, 10)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(queue.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(queue[0].status, ParkStatus::Dead);
    assert_eq!(
        queue[0].last_error,
        "the event is 101 bytes, above the sink's limit of 100"
    );
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_store_outage_keeps_parking_events_in_flight_until_it_ends() {
    let down = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
    let store = TestStore::default();
    store.fail_appends.store(u32::MAX, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let setup = parking_after(Duration::from_secs(1));
    let sink = ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, outage(&down, 0));
    let mut harness = Harness::start(setup, sink, store).await;
    harness.send(0, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(120)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.ack.inflight_events(), 1);
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"k").await, vec![]);
    harness.store.fail_appends.store(0, Ordering::SeqCst);
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(100));
    assert_eq!(
        harness.store.queue(b"k").await,
        vec![(0, ParkStatus::Retrying)]
    );
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn the_parked_limit_holds_events_in_flight_until_queues_drain() {
    let down = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
    let store = TestStore::default();
    store.seed(b"a", 0, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    store.seed(b"a", 1, ParkStatus::Retrying).await;
    let setup = Setup {
        park_after: Duration::from_secs(1),
        max_parked_events: 2,
        ..Setup::default()
    };
    let down_for = Arc::clone(&down);
    let script: Script = Box::new(move |_, _| {
        if down_for.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
            Outcome::Retryable(SinkError("throttled".to_owned()))
        } else {
            Outcome::Delivered
        }
    });
    let mut harness = Harness::start(setup, ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, script), store).await;
    harness.send(5, b"b", 10).unwrap();
    assert!(harness.quiet_for(Duration::from_secs(60)).await);
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"b").await, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(harness.ack.inflight_events(), 1);
    down.store(false, Ordering::SeqCst);
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(100));
    let mut delivered = harness.delivered(3).await;
    delivered.sort_unstable();
    assert_eq!(delivered, vec![0, 1, 5]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn park_all_parks_every_queued_event_and_stops_sending() {
    let script: Script = Box::new(|_, _| Outcome::Retryable(SinkError("down".to_owned())));
    let mut harness = Harness::new(2, 2, ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, script)).await;
    harness.send(0, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(1, b"k", 10).unwrap();
    harness.send(2, b"other", 10).unwrap();
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
    harness.lanes.park_all();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(3).await, Lsn(102));
    let sent = harness.sink.sends().len();
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(60)).await;
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.sends().len(), sent);
    assert_eq!(
        harness.store.queue(b"k").await,
        vec![(0, ParkStatus::Retrying), (1, ParkStatus::Retrying)]
    );
    assert_eq!(
        harness.store.queue(b"other").await,
        vec![(2, ParkStatus::Retrying)]
    );
}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/model.rs` (new file):

```rust
use super::*;

const KEYS: u8 = 4;
const EVENTS: usize = 30;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Fate {
    Flaky(u32),
    Terminal,
    Outage,
}

fn fate(code: u8) -> Fate {
    match code {
        0..=2 => Fate::Flaky(u32::from(code)),
        3 => Fate::Terminal,
        _ => Fate::Outage,
    }
}

fn script(fates: Vec<Fate>, outage_until: Instant) -> Script {
    Box::new(
        move |ordinal, attempt| match fates[usize::try_from(ordinal).unwrap()] {
            Fate::Flaky(failures) if attempt <= failures => {
                Outcome::Retryable(SinkError("flaky".to_owned()))
            }
            Fate::Terminal => Outcome::Terminal(SinkError("rejected".to_owned())),
            Fate::Outage if Instant::now() < outage_until => {
                Outcome::Retryable(SinkError("outage".to_owned()))
            }
            Fate::Flaky(_) | Fate::Outage => Outcome::Delivered,
        },
    )
}

fn check_order(sends: &[Sent], keys: &[u8]) -> Result<(), TestCaseError> {
    for key in 0..KEYS {
        let per_key: Vec<&Sent> = sends
            .iter()
            .filter(|sent| keys[usize::try_from(sent.ordinal).unwrap()] == key)
            .collect();
        for pair in per_key.windows(2) {
            prop_assert!(
                pair[0].ordinal <= pair[1].ordinal,
                "key {key}: {} after {}",
                pair[1].ordinal,
                pair[0].ordinal
            );
            if pair[0].ordinal != pair[1].ordinal {
                prop_assert!(
                    pair[0].delivered,
                    "key {key}: {} sent before {} was delivered",
                    pair[1].ordinal,
                    pair[0].ordinal
                );
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

async fn run_phase(
    store: TestStore,
    keys: &[u8],
    fates: &[Fate],
    outage_until: Instant,
    lanes: usize,
) -> Result<Harness, TestCaseError> {
    let setup = Setup {
        lanes,
        concurrency: 2,
        park_after: Duration::from_secs(1),
        ..Setup::default()
    };
    let sink = ScriptedSink::new(LIMITS, script(fates.to_vec(), outage_until));
    let mut harness = Harness::start(setup, sink, store).await;
    for (ordinal, key) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
        harness
            .send(u32::try_from(ordinal).unwrap(), &[*key], 10)
            .unwrap();
    }
    let last = Lsn(100 + u64::try_from(keys.len() - 1).unwrap());
    prop_assert_eq!(harness.settle(keys.len()).await, last);
    prop_assert_eq!(harness.ack.inflight_events(), 0);
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(120)).await;
    Ok(harness)
}

proptest! {
    #![proptest_config(ProptestConfig::with_cases(48))]
    #[test]
    fn o2_o3_a1_lanes_park_and_drain_under_random_failures_and_restarts(
        keys in proptest::collection::vec(0u8..KEYS, 1..EVENTS),
        codes in proptest::collection::vec(0u8..6, EVENTS),
        store_failures in 0u32..4,
        lanes in 1usize..3,
    ) {
        let fates: Vec<Fate> = codes.iter().copied().map(fate).collect();
        let mut dead_at: Vec<Option<usize>> = vec![None; usize::from(KEYS)];
        for (ordinal, key) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
            let slot = &mut dead_at[usize::from(*key)];
            if slot.is_none() && fates[ordinal] == Fate::Terminal {
                *slot = Some(ordinal);
            }
        }
        let blocked = |ordinal: usize| {
            dead_at[usize::from(keys[ordinal])].is_some_and(|dead| ordinal >= dead)
        };
        let runtime = tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
            .enable_time()
            .start_paused(true)
            .build()
            .unwrap();
        runtime.block_on(async {
            let outage_until = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
            let store = TestStore::default();
            store.fail_appends.store(store_failures, Ordering::SeqCst);
            let first = run_phase(store, &keys, &fates, outage_until, lanes).await?;
            let sends = first.sink.sends();
            check_order(&sends, &keys)?;
            let mut delivered: Vec<u32> = sends.iter().filter(|sent| sent.delivered).map(|sent| sent.ordinal).collect();
            delivered.sort_unstable();
            let expected: Vec<u32> = (0..keys.len())
                .filter(|ordinal| !blocked(*ordinal))
                .map(|ordinal| u32::try_from(ordinal).unwrap())
                .collect();
            prop_assert_eq!(&delivered, &expected, "each unblocked event is delivered exactly once");
            for key in 0..KEYS {
                let queue = first.store.queue(&[key]).await;
                let expected: Vec<(u32, ParkStatus)> = (0..keys.len())
                    .filter(|ordinal| keys[*ordinal] == key && blocked(*ordinal))
                    .map(|ordinal| {
                        let status = if Some(ordinal) == dead_at[usize::from(key)] {
                            ParkStatus::Dead
                        } else {
                            ParkStatus::Retrying
                        };
                        (u32::try_from(ordinal).unwrap(), status)
                    })
                    .collect();
                prop_assert_eq!(queue, expected, "key {}", key);
            }

            let restarted = TestStore {
                inner: first.store.inner.clone(),
                ..TestStore::default()
            };
            drop(first);
            let second = run_phase(restarted, &keys, &fates, outage_until, lanes).await?;
            let sends = second.sink.sends();
            check_order(&sends, &keys)?;
            for sent in &sends {
                prop_assert!(!blocked(usize::try_from(sent.ordinal).unwrap()), "blocked event {} was sent after the restart", sent.ordinal);
            }
            let mut delivered: Vec<u32> = sends.iter().filter(|sent| sent.delivered).map(|sent| sent.ordinal).collect();
            delivered.sort_unstable();
            let replayed: Vec<u32> = (0..keys.len())
                .filter(|ordinal| dead_at[usize::from(keys[*ordinal])].is_none())
                .map(|ordinal| u32::try_from(ordinal).unwrap())
                .collect();
            prop_assert_eq!(
                &delivered,
                &replayed,
                "only keys without a parked queue replay to the sink"
            );
            Ok(())
        })?;
    }
}
```

```diff
+        state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn >= written)
+    })
+    .await?;
+    session.set_mode(Mode::Deliver);
+    let events = session.next_events(3).await?;
+    let statuses: Vec<&Value> = events
+        .iter()
+        .map(|event| &event["after"]["status"])
+        .collect();
+    assert_eq!(statuses, ["new", "paid", "shipped"]);
+    wait_for_queue(&stores, 1, <[ParkedEvent]>::is_empty).await?;
+    session.stop().await
+}
+
+#[tokio::test]
+async fn a_rejected_event_is_parked_dead_and_blocks_only_its_key() -> TestResult {
+    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
+    orders(&pg).await?;
+    let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
+    let stores = support::filesystem_stores(dir.path());
+    let mut session = support::spawn_with_stores(
+        support::params(&pg),
+        keyed(Duration::from_secs(30)),
+        stores.clone(),
+    );
+    session.set_mode(Mode::RejectContaining("poison".to_owned()));
+    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'poison')")
+        .await?;
+    pg.execute("UPDATE orders SET status = 'later' WHERE id = 1")
+        .await?;
+    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (2, 'fine')").await?;
+    let written = pg.current_wal_lsn().await?;
+    let delivered = session.next_event().await?;
+    assert_eq!(delivered["after"]["id"], 2);
+    pg.wait_for_slot(SLOT, WAIT, |state| {
+        state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn >= written)
+    })
+    .await?;
+    let parked = wait_for_queue(&stores, 1, |queue| queue.len() == 2).await?;
+    let statuses: Vec<ParkStatus> = parked.iter().map(|event| event.status).collect();
+    assert_eq!(statuses, [ParkStatus::Dead, ParkStatus::Retrying]);
+    assert_eq!(parked[0].last_error, "test sink rejects the event");
+    assert!(session.no_event_within(Duration::from_secs(1)).await?);
+    session.stop().await
+}
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/park.rs` (new file):

```rust
use std::cmp::Reverse;

use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::{Failure, Key, Lane, Stored};
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;
use crate::sink::SinkError;
use crate::state::now_ms;

impl Key {
    pub(super) fn record_failure(&mut self, error: &SinkError) {
        let failure = self.failure.get_or_insert_with(|| Failure {
            since: Instant::now(),
            since_ms: now_ms(),
            attempts: 0,
            error: String::new(),
        });
        failure.attempts = failure.attempts.saturating_add(1);
        failure.error = error.to_string();
    }
}

impl Lane {
    pub(super) fn park_due(&self, state: &Key, now: Instant) -> bool {
        state
            .failure
            .as_ref()
            .is_some_and(|failure| now >= failure.since + self.park_after)
    }

    pub(super) fn retry_live(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, error: &SinkError) {
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        let id = state.queue.front().map(|head| head.outbound.id);
        if state.failure.is_none() {
            tracing::warn!(order_key = %key, ?id, %error, "delivery failed; retrying");
        } else {
            tracing::debug!(order_key = %key, ?id, %error, "delivery failed again; retrying");
        }
        state.record_failure(error);
        let now = Instant::now();
        let Some(since) = state.failure.as_ref().map(|failure| failure.since) else {
            return;
        };
        let park_at = since + self.park_after;
        if now >= park_at {
            self.start_parking(key, false);
            return;
        }
        let due = (now + state.backoff.next_delay()).min(park_at);
        self.retries.push(Reverse((due, key)));
    }

    pub(super) fn start_parking(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, dead: bool) {
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        if state.stored.is_some() || state.queue.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let floor = state.queue.front().map(|pending| pending.outbound.id);
        state.stored = Some(Stored {
            floor,
            dead_on_append: dead,
            ..Stored::default()
        });
        tracing::warn!(order_key = %key, id = ?floor, dead, "parking an order key's events");
        self.to_append.insert(key);
        if !dead && !self.closing {
            let delay = state.backoff.next_delay();
            self.retries.push(Reverse((Instant::now() + delay, key)));
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn oversize(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, bytes: usize) {
        let error = SinkError(format!(
            "the event is {bytes} bytes, above the sink's limit of {}",
            self.capabilities.max_message_bytes
        ));
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        if let Some(stored) = state.stored.as_mut() {
            if let Some(head) = stored.head.take() {
                self.heads_in_memory = self.heads_in_memory.saturating_sub(1);
                tracing::error!(order_key = %key, id = %head.id, %error, "a parked event is too large to send; marking it dead");
                self.to_kill.push((key, head.id));
            }
        } else {
            tracing::error!(order_key = %key, %error, "an event is too large to send; parking it dead");
            state.record_failure(&error);
            self.start_parking(key, true);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn park_all(&mut self) {
        self.closing = true;
        self.ready.clear();
        let keys: Vec<OrderKeyId> = self
            .keys
            .iter()
            .filter(|(key, state)| state.stored.is_none() && self.is_loaded(**key))
            .map(|(key, _)| *key)
            .collect();
        for key in keys {
            self.start_parking(key, false);
        }
    }
}
```

---

### Task 10: Oversize events as pointers

**Depends on:** Task 4, Task 9
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** high

Completes spec §7.5 (R62): with a payload store, a head above the sink's `max_message_bytes` is queued for offload instead of parking dead. The lane `put`s the encoded body, builds the pointer with `pointer_body`, replaces the head's body with it (keeping the attributes and the ticket) and makes the key ready. A `put` failure is a retryable failure of the key (a parked head backs off and is re-read); a pointer still above the limit, or a body that is not a JSON object, parks the event dead through `give_up`. Drained oversize heads take the same path.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/mod.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/park.rs`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/offload.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/mod.rs`
- Create: `crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/tests/offload.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/parking.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PayloadStore`, `PayloadRef`, `pointer_body`, `S3PayloadStore` (Task 4); `Stores::payload` (Task 7); `Lane::front` (Task 8); `Lane::{oversize, retry_live, start_parking}`, `Key::record_failure` (Task 9); `support::{spawn_full, LIMITS, memory_stores}`.

```rust
use super::*;

fn limited(max_message_bytes: usize) -> ScriptedSink {
    let limits = SinkCapabilities {
        max_message_bytes,
        ..LIMITS
    };
    ScriptedSink::new(limits, Box::new(|_, _| Outcome::Delivered))
}

fn pointer(ordinal: u32, bytes: usize) -> String {
    let id = EventId::new(Lsn(0x10), ordinal);
    format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{id}","op":"insert","key":{{"id":1}},"payload_ref":{{"uri":"memory://{id}","bytes":{bytes}}},"source":{{"pipeline":"p"}}}}"#
    )
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn an_oversize_event_is_sent_as_a_pointer_to_its_stored_payload() {
    let payloads = Arc::new(TestPayloads::default());
    let mut harness = Harness::start_with_payloads(
        Setup::default(),
        limited(300),
        TestStore::default(),
        Some(Arc::clone(&payloads)),
    )
    .await;
    let full = envelope(0, 400);
    harness.send_body(0, b"k", full.clone()).unwrap();
    harness.send(1, b"small", 10).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(2).await, Lsn(101));
    let id = EventId::new(Lsn(0x10), 0);
    assert_eq!(payloads.stored.lock().unwrap().get(&id), Some(&full));
    let bodies = harness.sink.bodies.lock().unwrap().clone();
    assert_eq!(
        std::str::from_utf8(&bodies[&0]).unwrap(),
        pointer(0, full.len())
    );
    assert_eq!(bodies[&1], Bytes::from(vec![b'x'; 10]));
    assert_eq!(harness.store.inner.parked_events(), 0);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_payload_store_outage_retries_and_then_parks_the_full_event() {
    let payloads = Arc::new(TestPayloads::default());
    payloads.fail.store(u32::MAX, Ordering::SeqCst);
    let setup = Setup {
        park_after: Duration::from_secs(1),
        ..Setup::default()
    };
    let mut harness =
        Harness::start_with_payloads(setup, limited(300), TestStore::default(), Some(payloads))
            .await;
    let full = envelope(0, 400);
    harness.send_body(0, b"k", full.clone()).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(100));
    let queue = harness
        .store
        .inner
        .read_parked(&OrderKeyId::of(b"k"), None, 10)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(queue.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(queue[0].status, ParkStatus::Retrying);
    assert_eq!(queue[0].body, full);
    assert!(
        queue[0]
            .last_error
            .starts_with("cannot store the oversize payload"),
        "{}",
        queue[0].last_error
    );
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.sends(), vec![]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_pointer_still_above_the_limit_is_parked_dead() {
    let mut harness = Harness::start_with_payloads(
        Setup::default(),
        limited(120),
        TestStore::default(),
        Some(Arc::new(TestPayloads::default())),
    )
    .await;
    harness.send_body(0, b"k", envelope(0, 400)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(harness.settle(1).await, Lsn(100));
    let queue = harness
        .store
        .inner
        .read_parked(&OrderKeyId::of(b"k"), None, 10)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(queue[0].status, ParkStatus::Dead);
    assert!(
        queue[0].last_error.starts_with("the pointer event is"),
        "{}",
        queue[0].last_error
    );
    assert_eq!(harness.sink.sends(), vec![]);
}

#[tokio::test(start_paused = true)]
async fn a_parked_oversize_event_drains_as_a_pointer() {
    let store = TestStore::default();
    let full = envelope(3, 400);
    store
        .inner
        .append_parked(&[ParkedEvent {
            body: full.clone(),
            ..parked(b"k", 3, ParkStatus::Retrying)
        }])
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let payloads = Arc::new(TestPayloads::default());
    let harness = Harness::start_with_payloads(
        Setup::default(),
        limited(300),
        store,
        Some(Arc::clone(&payloads)),
    )
    .await;
    assert_eq!(harness.delivered(1).await, vec![3]);
    let bodies = harness.sink.bodies.lock().unwrap().clone();
    assert_eq!(
        std::str::from_utf8(&bodies[&3]).unwrap(),
        pointer(3, full.len())
    );
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(1)).await;
    assert_eq!(harness.store.queue(b"k").await, vec![]);
    assert_eq!(payloads.stored.lock().unwrap().len(), 1);
}
```

```diff
     pub(super) fn oversize(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, bytes: usize) {

+            if !self.to_offload.contains(&key) {
+                self.to_offload.push_back(key);
+            }
+            return;
+        }
         let error = SinkError(format!(
```

`crates/pg-cdc/src/lane/offload.rs` (new file):

```rust
use std::cmp::Reverse;

use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::Lane;
use crate::order::OrderKeyId;
use crate::payload::pointer_body;
use crate::sink::{Outbound, SinkError};

impl Lane {
    pub(super) async fn offload(&mut self) {
        let Some(key) = self.to_offload.pop_front() else {
            return;
        };
        let (Some(payload), Some(head)) = (self.payload.clone(), self.front(&key).cloned()) else {
            return;
        };
        let reference = match payload.put(&head.id, &head.body).await {
            Ok(reference) => reference,
            Err(error) => {
                let error = SinkError(format!("cannot store the oversize payload: {error}"));
                self.offload_failed(key, &error);
                return;
            }
        };
        let body = match pointer_body(&head.body, &reference) {
            Ok(body) => body,
            Err(error) => {
                let error = SinkError(format!("cannot build a pointer event: {error}"));
                self.give_up(key, &error);
                return;
            }
        };
        let pointer = Outbound { body, ..head };
        let bytes = pointer.encoded_len();
        if bytes > self.capabilities.max_message_bytes {
            let error = SinkError(format!(
                "the pointer event is {bytes} bytes, above the sink's limit of {}",
                self.capabilities.max_message_bytes
            ));
            self.give_up(key, &error);
            return;
        }
        tracing::info!(order_key = %key, id = %pointer.id, uri = %reference.uri, bytes = reference.bytes, "sending an oversize event as a pointer");
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        match state.stored.as_mut() {
            Some(stored) => stored.head = Some(pointer),
            None => {
                if let Some(front) = state.queue.front_mut() {
                    front.outbound = pointer;
                }
            }
        }
        self.ready.push_back(key);
    }

    fn offload_failed(&mut self, key: OrderKeyId, error: &SinkError) {
        let Some(state) = self.keys.get_mut(&key) else {
            return;
        };
        if let Some(stored) = state.stored.as_mut() {
            if stored.head.take().is_some() {
                self.heads_in_memory = self.heads_in_memory.saturating_sub(1);
            }
            tracing::warn!(order_key = %key, %error, "retrying a parked oversize event");
            state.record_failure(error);
            let delay = state.backoff.next_delay();
            self.retries.push(Reverse((Instant::now() + delay, key)));
        } else {
            self.retry_live(key, error);
        }
    }
}
```

### Task 11: Park the remainder at shutdown, end to end

**Depends on:** Task 7, Task 10
**Complexity:** standard
**Risk:** high

Implements spec §12.5 step 2 (R63): when the 5 s drain leaves events unsettled, the session calls `Lanes::park_all` and waits at most another 5 s, then sends the final status update and returns as before. Two milestone 2 tests change meaning (R72): the session shutdown test keeps proving that what can neither deliver nor park replays, now with a store that refuses appends, and the CLI EPIPE test now expects the undelivered event parked on disk and confirmed. New end-to-end tests run a shutdown that parks and a second session that drains, and run `pg-cdc run` against moto with the topic deleted mid-run so the event parks to the filesystem store and the slot advances. The README documents milestone 3.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/src/session.rs`
- Modify: `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/support.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/session.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/stream/parking.rs`, `crates/pg-cdc/tests/cli.rs`
- Modify: `README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Lanes::park_all` (Task 9); `session::DRAIN_TIMEOUT`; `support::{filesystem_stores, parked, spawn_with_stores}`.
- Produces: `Session::settle_within` (private); stream harness `UnparkableStore` and `support::unparkable_stores() -> Stores`; in `tests/cli.rs`, `ConfigFile::{state, parked_files(), wait_for_parked(count)}`.

```diff
+#[tokio::test]
+async fn shutdown_parks_what_the_sink_did_not_take_and_the_next_session_drains_it() -> TestResult {
+    let pg = Postgres::start().await?;
+    orders(&pg).await?;
+    let dir = tempfile::tempdir()?;
+    let stores = support::filesystem_stores(dir.path());
+    let mut first = support::spawn_with_stores(
+        support::params(&pg),
+        keyed(Duration::from_secs(3600)),
+        stores.clone(),
+    );
+    first.set_mode(Mode::Fail);
+    pg.execute("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'undelivered')")
+        .await?;
+    let written = pg.current_wal_lsn().await?;
+    first.wait_for_failures(1).await?;
+    let stopping = tokio::time::Instant::now();
+    first.stop().await?;
+    assert!(
+        stopping.elapsed() < DRAIN_TIMEOUT * 2 + Duration::from_secs(5),
+        "shutdown took {:?}",
+        stopping.elapsed()
+    );
+    let parked = support::parked(&stores, &support::order_key(1)).await?;
+    assert_eq!(parked.len(), 1);
+    let state = pg.wait_for_slot(SLOT, WAIT, |state| !state.active).await?;
+    assert!(
+        state.confirmed_flush_lsn.is_some_and(|lsn| lsn >= written),
+        "the parked transaction was not confirmed"
+    );
+    let mut second = support::spawn_with_stores(
+        support::params(&pg),
+        keyed(Duration::from_secs(3600)),
+        stores.clone(),
+    );
+    let drained = second.next_event().await?;
+    assert_eq!(drained["after"]["status"], "undelivered");
+    assert!(second.no_event_within(Duration::from_secs(1)).await?);
+    wait_for_queue(&stores, 1, <[ParkedEvent]>::is_empty).await?;
+    second.stop().await
+}
```

`README.md`:

````diff
--- a/README.md
+++ b/README.md
@@ -2,7 +2,7 @@
 
 pg-cdc streams row changes for the tables in one Postgres publication through a logical replication slot to a sink. Delivery is at least once, and pg-cdc never writes to the source database.
 
-Status: milestone 2 of the [design](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md). Changes are delivered to SNS, stdout or a discard sink through ordered lanes with retries, and the slot is acknowledged once every event of a transaction, and of every transaction before it, is delivered. Parking, leadership and the admin API arrive in later milestones.
+Status: milestone 3 of the [design](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-pg-cdc-design.md). Changes are delivered to SNS, stdout or a discard sink through ordered lanes with retries. An event the sink cannot take within `delivery.park_after` is parked in a state store (S3 or a local directory), so the slot keeps advancing during a sink outage, and parked events are redelivered in order once the sink recovers. Leadership and the admin API arrive in later milestones.
 
 ## Run

@@ -46,10 +57,21 @@
 
 The SNS message limit comes from the topic's `MaximumMessageSize` attribute when `GetTopicAttributes` returns it, and is 256 KiB otherwise; the `SNS topic ready` log at startup shows the limit in use and where it came from. Topics above 256 KiB have not been verified against real AWS. pg-cdc keeps retrying the sink preflight and does not open the replication connection until it succeeds.
 
+## Parking
+
+- **When an event parks.** An event still undelivered `delivery.park_after` (default 30 s) after its first failure is written to the state store, together with every later event of its order key, and counts as settled, so the slot moves on. An event the sink rejects for good (SNS `SenderFault`) is parked at once as `dead`.
+- **Parked keys stay in order.** While an order key has parked events, its new events are appended behind them and never sent ahead. pg-cdc redelivers the queue from the store, oldest first, and the key returns to the live path once its queue is empty. Other keys keep flowing throughout.
+- **Dead events block their key** until an operator removes them. Until the admin API (milestone 5), stop pg-cdc, delete the dead event's object (`<prefix>/<pipeline>/parked/<order key id>/<event id>.json` in S3, or the same path under `state_store.path`), and start it again.
+- **Oversize events.** An event larger than the sink's message limit is stored in the payload store, and the sink receives a pointer instead: the same envelope without `before`, `after` and `unchanged_toast`, plus `"payload_ref": {"uri": "s3://bucket/prefix/pipeline/payloads/<event id>.json", "bytes": 912345}`. Without a `payload_store`, such an event is parked as dead. A lifecycle rule should expire payloads after your consumers' retention period.
+- **Startup.** Reading starts at once; delivery for an order key waits until the parked queue heads of its partition (the first byte of the order key id, 256 partitions) have loaded. Replayed events below a queue's head are skipped. Store calls are bounded by `state_store.max_concurrency` (default 32), including listing during head loading.
+- **Shutdown.** On SIGTERM, pg-cdc stops reading, gives in-flight events 5 s to deliver, parks whatever remains (another 5 s at most), sends a final status update and exits 0. Events that could neither deliver nor park replay on the next start.
+- **Limits.** At `delivery.max_parked_events` (default 10,000,000; `0` is unlimited), or while the state store is unavailable, events stay in flight and the reader stops at `delivery.max_inflight_bytes`. pg-cdc checks that the state store answers before it opens the replication connection.
+- **State stores.** `state_store: { type: s3, bucket, prefix, region }` uses conditional writes and suits active/standby across hosts; the bucket should use default encryption, because parked objects hold row data. `state_store: { type: filesystem, path: /var/lib/pg-cdc }` keeps the same tree on local disk and suits a single host.
+
 ## Limits in this milestone
 
-- **A rejected event blocks its order key.** When the sink rejects an event for good (SNS `SenderFault`), or an event is larger than the sink's message limit, pg-cdc logs it at error once and never sends it again. Later events of the same order key wait behind it, other keys keep flowing, and the slot stops advancing at that event, so WAL accumulates until pg-cdc is restarted, which replays it. Nothing is lost or skipped. Milestone 3 parks such events in a state store instead.
-- **Memory** is bounded by `delivery.max_inflight_bytes`, measured on encoded event bodies, plus one event above the bound and up to 65 raw replication messages read ahead of the bound. The read-ahead is a fixed number of messages, independent of transaction size, so its size depends on the widest row.
+- **Memory** is bounded by `delivery.max_inflight_bytes`, measured on encoded event bodies, plus one event above the bound and up to 65 raw replication messages read ahead of the bound, plus at most `delivery.lanes` x 10 parked events loaded for redelivery (one SNS batch per lane). The read-ahead is a fixed number of messages, independent of transaction size, so its size depends on the widest row.
+- **The parked count is approximate.** It is loaded from the store at startup and can over-count replayed events until the next restart, so the limit errs towards stalling early.
 
 `delivery.order_by` is `key` (default), `table`, `column:<name>` or `pipeline`. `sink: { type: stdout }` writes each change as a JSON line on stdout and logs to stderr; `sink: { type: discard }` drops every change, for load tests. With the SNS and discard sinks, logs are JSON on stdout. Any key can be overridden with `PGCDC__<SECTION>__<KEY>` (lists as comma-separated values), and passwords come only from the environment. For Aurora with RDS IAM auth, use `credentials: { type: aws_rds_iam }` with the default AWS credential chain. pg-cdc runs on Linux and macOS.
 
````
