## Self-Review

### Spec coverage for milestone 3 (spec §19 item 3)

| Spec section | Requirement | Task |
|---|---|---|
| §1, §2.1 goals 2 and 5, §4 criteria 1 and 3 | an event that cannot be delivered within `park_after` is parked in a durable store and the slot keeps advancing; nothing is lost | 9, 11 (`parking_lets_the_slot_advance_while_the_sink_fails_and_drains_in_order`, `a_deleted_topic_parks_events_on_disk_and_the_slot_advances`) |
| §4 criterion 6 | memory within `max_inflight_bytes` plus fixed overhead | 8 (one batch of parked heads per lane, R60), 11 (README) |
| §5 | StateStore and PayloadStore adapters behind the lanes; lanes drain parked queues | 1–4, 7–10 |
| §7.1, §7.5 | `payload_ref` on a pointer event; the full envelope in the PayloadStore; without one the event is dead; threshold on body plus attributes | 4, 10 |
| §8 | lanes own per-key parked state without locks; one event per key in flight; retries do not block the lane | 8, 9 |
| §9.1 | an event settles on delivery or a confirmed park, nothing else (A1) | 8 (`Ticket::park`, `Ticket::skip_replayed`), 9 |
| §9.4, §10.5 | at the parked limit or with the store down, events stay in flight and the reader stops at the byte bound | 9 (`the_parked_limit_holds_events_in_flight_until_queues_drain`, `a_store_outage_keeps_parking_events_in_flight_until_it_ends`) |
| §10.2 | park after `park_after` from the first failure; a terminal outcome parks at once as dead; no outage mode | 9 |
| §10.3 | one queue per key in id order; new events queue behind; the lane drains head by head with §10.1 backoff; live again once the store confirms the queue empty | 8, 9 |
| §10.4 | a dead event blocks its key until an operator acts (admin API in milestone 5, manual removal documented) | 8, 9, 11 (README), R73 |
| §10.5 | `max_parked_events` (default 10,000,000, 0 unlimited) | 5, 9 |
| §10.6 | reading starts at once; heads load per partition (256, first byte of the order key id) bounded by `state_store.max_concurrency`; delivery waits for its partition; replays below the head are skipped; other replays append create-only | 8 |
| §11.3 | the trait with the exact signatures; `ParkedEvent`, `ParkedHead`, `LeaderRecord`; the seven-item contract checked by a shared suite | 1, 2, 3 |
| §11.4 | S3 layout, `If-None-Match: *` with 412 as success, partition listing, `StartAfter` reads, status rewrite, leader `If-Match`/`If-None-Match`, 5xx retried with backoff | 3 |
| §11.5 | filesystem tree, temp file plus `link()` plus directory fsync, leader compare-and-swap under a lock with the version in the record | 2 |
| §11.6 | PayloadStore trait, S3 at `<prefix>/<pipeline>/payloads/<event id>.json`, `s3://` URI and size; optional | 4, 5, 7 |
| §12.5 step 2 | park whatever remains after the drain | 11 |
| §14.2–§14.3, §14.6 | O2, O3, A1, A2 pinned; model-based property test of lanes and parking with random failures and restarts | see "Invariants Pinned in This Milestone"; 9 |
| §15 | `delivery.park_after`, `delivery.max_parked_events`, `state_store` (`s3`, `filesystem`, `max_concurrency`), `payload_store`; defaults; environment overrides | 5, 7 |
| §16.2 (store check) | the state store must answer before the task streams | 7 (R56) |
| §18.2, §18.4 | S3 on the AWS emulator (moto, R3); oversize events with and without a PayloadStore; StateStore suite on the filesystem store locally and on S3 | 1–4, 10 |
| Milestone 2 follow-ups | `limit_from_topic` from the parsed value; `is_fatal` pins; attribute characters SNS rejects | 6, 7 |

Deferred by design: the leader record's use in takeover and handover, `leadership.drain_timeout`, startup validation and `pg-cdc health` (milestone 4; the record's storage and compare-and-swap are built and tested here); the admin API, redrive and discard, the `parked` CLI, metrics and spans for parking and draining, the `chaos` wrapper and load tests (milestone 5); the real-AWS checks of R73.

### Placeholder scan

No step says "TBD", "TODO", "handle edge cases", "similar to Task N" or "write tests for the above". Every new file is given in full and every modified file as a unified diff against the state the previous task leaves it in; the one mechanical step (moving the milestone 2 lane tests in Task 8) gives the exact script, followed by the diff to apply to its output. The only prose-only edits are a manifest line (`aws-sdk-s3.workspace = true`, Tasks 3 and 4), creating three empty files (Task 1), and deleting `lane.rs` (Task 8).

### Type consistency

Checked across tasks against the assembled code: `EventId: FromStr`, `InvalidEventId`, `OrderKeyId: FromStr`, `OrderKeyId::partition`, `StateStore`, `ParkStatus`, `ParkedEvent::outbound`, `ParkedHead`, `LeaderRecord`, `LeaderState`, `Version`, `Versioned`, `SwapOutcome`, `StoreError`, `store_root`, `parked_name`, `parse_parked_name`, `now_ms`, `encode_parked`, `decode_parked`, `decode_leader`, `PARTITIONS`, `MemoryStateStore` (Task 1); `FilesystemStateStore::new(&Path, &str, usize)` (Task 2); `S3StateStore::new(Client, String, &str, &str, usize)`, `s3_client(Option<String>)` (Task 3); `PayloadStore::put(&EventId, &[u8])`, `PayloadRef { uri, bytes }`, `S3PayloadStore::new(Client, String, &str, &str)`, `pointer_body(&[u8], &PayloadRef)` (Task 4); `StateStoreConfig`, `PayloadStoreConfig`, `DeliveryConfig::{park_after, max_parked_events}` (Task 5); `Stores`, `LaneSettings`, `Lanes::spawn(LaneSettings, &Arc<dyn Sink>, &Stores, &Arc<Semaphore>, &UnboundedSender<Report>)`, `SessionConfig::lane_settings`, `run_session(.., &Stores, ..)`, `store_ready` (Task 7); `Ticket::{park, skip_replayed}` and the lane internals (Task 8); `Lanes::park_all`, `Failure`, `start_parking`, `oversize` (Task 9); `give_up`, `offload` (Task 10). Every consumer names its producer's exact signature, and the assembled tree compiled with clippy `-D warnings` and passed the workspace's tests at every task boundary.

### Review Focus check

Each of the five Review Focus items names its tests, and each test sits in the task that owns the code: a sink and store outage together (Tasks 8, 9 and 11), a crash between parking and confirmation followed by a replay (Tasks 8, 9 and 11), a large parked backlog and S3 request bounds (Tasks 3 and 8), wide rows with and without a working payload bucket (Tasks 4 and 10), and a mistyped state bucket at deploy (Tasks 3 and 7). Other inputs considered and already covered: a stored object that is not valid JSON or names an unknown attribute type (reported as `Malformed` with its name: Task 1 `malformed_objects_are_reported_with_their_name`); stray and temp files in the filesystem tree (Task 2); a concurrent leader swap race (exactly one winner: Task 1 suite); a drained head the sink now rejects (marked dead: Task 8 `a_terminal_outcome_on_a_parked_head_marks_it_dead`); a key whose events arrive while its partition is still loading (Task 8); `park_all` while the sink is down (Task 9).
